   479	
   480	## 7. `orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/smoke.py` — blueprint
   481	
   482	Modeled line-for-line on T14's smoke.py (in-process uvicorn on a daemon thread, temp dir + DB, `PLAN_TEST_MODE=1`, `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS=50`, config via `load_config(path=None, env=...)`, `create_schema`, `build_adapters`, `create_app`, `free_or(port)`, `wait_ready`, headless chromium via `playwright.sync_api`, numbered `ok NN` prints, try/finally teardown closing client/page/browser/playwright/server and removing the temp dir, exit 0 + final `SMOKE PASS`). Run: `cd <repo> && .venv/bin/python orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/smoke.py`.
   483	
   484	### Setup specifics (the three clock facts that make this deterministic)
   485	
   486	1. **`PLAN_FAKE_NOW` = the REAL current local date at T12:00:00** — `datetime.now().astimezone().date().isoformat() + "T12:00:00"`. Reason: `seed_demo` computes "today" from `datetime.now()` (demo.py:30), so fake-now must share that calendar date for `/api/day/today` (planning date of fake noon = that date, §6.1) to hit the demo day. Noon is safe at any real run time: planning date at 12:00 is always the calendar date.
   487	2. **Seed** via `planner.seed.demo.seed_demo(conn)` on a direct connection (after `create_schema`), then **normalize seed timestamps** on the same connection: `noon = int(datetime.fromisoformat(today + "T12:00:00").astimezone().timestamp())`; `conn.execute("UPDATE tickets SET created_at = ?, updated_at = ?", (noon - 600, noon - 600))` + commit. Why: `seed_demo` stamps real wall time (demo.py:29) while the TestClock is frozen at fake noon (clock.py:27-41) — without this, t5's review `waiting_since` (the updated_at fallback: views.py:297-309; demo emits no `state_changed` events, demo.py:202-206) could land after the proposals filed at fake noon and break "t5 is oldest".
   488	3. **Space the (d) filings with `POST /api/test/set-now`** (testmode.py:53-70): the frozen clock would otherwise give every proposal the identical `waiting_since` second, leaving the oldest-first order to an unstable tie-break (`digest.sort` on equal keys falls back to build order = `ORDER BY id` over random slugs — views.py:270-272/310, ids.py:22-27).
   489	
   490	Resolve demo ids up front via httpx: day view (`GET /api/day/today`) → `t4, t8, t5 = tickets[0..2].id` (seed order, demo.py:247-255; identify also by title as a guard); `t1` = the single `GET /api/tickets?state=needs_success` result; the todo item = the single `GET /api/items?status=todo` result ("Research the search index options.").
   491	
   492	All DOM waits use `page.wait_for_selector` / `page.wait_for_function` (flush counters via `window.__plannerDebug.flushes` where a re-render must be awaited) — **never bare sleeps for DOM state**.
   493	
   494	### Checks (each one `ok NN` line)
   495	
   496	**(a) Day renders** — `page.goto(base)"/"` → hash `#/day`; wait `.screen[data-screen="day"]`; assert: brief text "Demo day: ship the feature slice…" visible; `[data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus` = "Ship the demo feature slice."; exactly 2 `[data-node]` child rows (`"0"`, `"1"`; statuses accepted/proposed via `data-status`); 3 `[data-ticket-id]` rows in order `[t4, t8, t5]` (attribute list comparison); `[data-pending-count]` value == `"1"` (t5's review entry is the only approval); `[data-chat-panel]` present with `[data-chat-input]` and `[data-chat-send]`.
   497	
   498	**(b) Remove + Accept-all (the "accept-all adds children to the day list" proof)** —
   499	1. Click `[data-ticket-id="<t8>"] [data-remove]` → wait for 2 `[data-ticket-id]` rows (`wait_for_function` on `querySelectorAll` length; the removal event flushes the re-render). Order now `[t4, t5]`.
   500	2. Click `[data-accept-all]` → wait for 3 rows; assert order exactly `[t4, t5, t8]` — t8 re-added at the END; assert all three `[data-node]` rows have `data-status="accepted"` (root + both children). Cross-check via API: `GET /api/day/today` → `plan.root.status == "accepted"`, both children `"accepted"`, `[t["id"] for t in tickets] == [t4, t5, t8]`.
   501	
   502	**(c) Day chat echo + flush survival** — `page.fill('[data-chat-input]', "hello planner")`; click `[data-chat-send]`; wait for `[data-chat-msg="you"]` (text "hello planner") and `[data-chat-msg="planner"]` containing "echo: hello planner" (EchoGatewayAdapter, fakes.py:94-99). The first send persists a session key + logs `chat_session_created` → a flush re-renders the whole screen: wait for the flush counter to increment past its pre-send value, then assert BOTH messages are still present (module-scope transcript survived the full re-render). API: `GET /api/day/today` → `chat_session_key == "fake-sess-1"`.
   503	
   504	**(d) Review setup via API (httpx, agent header where stated)** — all POSTs asserted 200:
   505	1. At fake 12:00:00 — `POST /api/tickets/{t1}/propose/success` body `{"body": "Agent-drafted success criteria."}`, header `X-Plan-Actor: agent` (plain-agent classification: authctx.py:57-65; parks because t1 sits at its ceiling `needs_success` with `at_cap=propose`, demo.py:56-61).
   506	2. `POST /api/test/set-now` `{"now": today + "T12:00:30"}` → create fresh ticket `POST /api/tickets` `{"title": "Fresh smoke ticket"}` (no headers → human; R2 default grant ceiling=needs_success/propose) → `POST /api/tickets/{fresh}/propose/success` `{"body": "Fresh proposal body."}` with the agent header (parks identically).
   507	3. `POST /api/test/set-now` `{"now": today + "T12:01:00"}` → `POST /api/items/{item}/propose-status` `{"to": "done"}` with the agent header (sprints/api.py:234-241).
   508	4. `GET /api/queues` → approvals length 4, order `[t5(review), t1(success), fresh(success), item(status)]` — waiting_since noon-600 < 12:00:00 < 12:00:30 < 12:01:00.
   509	
   510	**(e) Review walk, oldest first** — `page.goto(base + "/#/review")`:
   511	1. **Card 1 = t5**: wait `[data-review-card][data-entity-id="<t5>"]`; `data-kind="review"`; `[data-approve]` present; NO `[data-grant-ceiling]`, NO `[data-edit]` on this card; result value + review notes text rendered. **Skip**: click `[data-skip]` → wait card for t1 (skip memory advanced, no server write). **Reload**: `page.reload()` → wait card for t5 again (skip memory is per-session; reload cleared it — t5 is back first). **Approve**: click `[data-approve]` → wait card for t1 (the approval's flush refetched the queue). API: t5 `state == "done"`.
   512	2. **Card 2 = t1** (`data-kind="success"`, `data-field="success"`): assert `[data-review-card] [data-accept]` is disabled with nothing picked; `select_option('[data-grant-ceiling]', "none")` → still disabled (one half only); `check('[data-grant-atcap] input[value="stop"]')` → enabled (both halves). Click Accept → wait card for fresh. API on t1: `state == "needs_approach"`, `ceiling == "needs_approach"` ("No further" pins the ceiling to the newly entered state, machine.py:87-89), `at_cap == "stop"`, `fields.success.value == "Agent-drafted success criteria."`.
   513	3. **Card 3 = fresh**: assert `[data-edit]` value == `"Fresh proposal body."` (prefill); fill with `"Human-edited body. "` (note the deliberate trailing space — exactness check); this time pick the radio FIRST: `check(... input[value="propose"])` → Accept still disabled (the other single-half order); `select_option('[data-grant-ceiling]', "needs_plan")` → enabled. Click Accept → wait card for the item. API on fresh: `fields.success.value == "Human-edited body. "` **exactly** (edit-accept stores the altered text verbatim, resolution.py:161), `ceiling == "needs_plan"`, `at_cap == "propose"`, `state == "needs_approach"`.
   514	4. **Card 4 = item** (`data-kind="status"`, no grant picker, no textarea): click `[data-accept-status]` → wait `[data-review-empty]` (queue empty → quiet line). API: item `status == "done"`, `status_proposal == null`. Assert the nav badge is hidden (`.nav-badge.hidden` present / `classList` contains `hidden`).
   515	
   516	**(f) Offline instance** — build a SECOND config/app/uvicorn on another port with its own temp DB, same `PLAN_FAKE_NOW`, plus `PLAN_GATEWAY_ADAPTER="offline"` (registry.py:61-69 → `OfflineGatewayAdapter`, fakes.py:102-108); `create_schema` + `seed_demo` + the same timestamp normalization; new browser page → `#/day`: wait `[data-chat-offline]`; assert brief text renders and 3 `[data-ticket-id]` rows exist (rest of the screen intact); assert NO `[data-chat-send]` (no input source when offline). Teardown stops both servers.
   517	
   518	---
   519	
   520	## 8. Risks and edge cases — each with its decided handling
   521	
   522	1. **Mid-interaction WS flush wipes picker/textarea state (Review).** Any unrelated event (dispatcher tick, another agent's proposal) flushes → full re-render → the grant picker resets and the quick-edit re-prefills. **Decision: acceptable v1.** Rationale: the re-render-everything doctrine is T14 law; preserving per-card edit state would need a keyed store invalidated on proposal supersession — machinery §15's spirit rejects for v1. The review surface is one-at-a-time with short dwell; the human's own actions flush only *after* the card resolves. Recorded here so stage-6 flake triage starts from "known, accepted".
   523	2. **Mid-typing flush wipes the day chat draft.** This one is NOT accepted — the Day screen self-flushes on every action, so the draft dies constantly without help. **Decision: mitigated** via `chatDrafts` (module scope; §1.7): the typed source reports every change (`ctx.onInput`), the rebuilt panel prefills from the store. Transient UI state, sanctioned pattern.
   524	3. **Result-field accepts where ceiling=done route to done.** `advanceTarget("in_progress", "done") === "done"` — the picker then offers exactly `No further` + `done`, matching the server floor (`resolve_grant(done, …)`, machine.py:94-99); the pair remains mandatory (§4.4.7). Covered by the helper mirror (§1.1); no special-case UI.
   525	4. **Entity vanished between queue fetch and detail fetch.** Detail rejection → mark in `vanished` (never wrap-reset) → re-render refetches the queue. No loop: `vanished` filters the entry even if a stale queue briefly re-lists it; genuinely resolved entries drop from the next queue payload (the approvals view derives from live rows, views.py:269-325). Same path handles the staleness guard (proposal superseded-to-resolved / state moved on) in §3.3.
   526	5. **Day with `plan: null` vs plan with zero children.** `null` → the Plan panel renders `quietLine("(no plan)")` (reject-all sets plan NULL, tree.py:147-153 — this is the normal post-reject state). A tree with zero children renders the root row + top actions only (accept-all on zero children is a valid server call, tree.py:101-114). Both explicitly built, neither instructional.
   527	6. **Chat entity id for a day.** Always `day.id` from the `/api/day/today` response (`day_YYYY-MM-DD`, days/api.py:71-80) — never constructed from the browser clock (the browser knows nothing about the 05:00 boundary rule). Mutating day routes reuse `day.id.slice(4)` for the same reason (§2).
   528	7. **Reply lands while the panel is detached** (flush replaced the screen mid-send). The reply is appended to the module store unconditionally; repaint is guarded by `root.isConnected`. The message appears on the next render (next flush or navigation). No lost data, no detached-DOM writes.
   529	8. **Double-fire in the flush gap.** Every mutating button disables itself from click until its promise settles AND the re-render replaces it; the ~250ms debounce window cannot re-submit.
   530	9. **`data-screen` collision with nav links** (components.js:53). Contract states the scoped selector (`.screen[data-screen=…]`); smoke uses it, stage-6 must too (§6 caveat row).
   531	10. **Frozen-clock ordering in smoke** (three interacting facts): seed stamps real time, TestClock is frozen, approvals tie-break is unstable at equal seconds. Handled in §7 setup (normalization + set-now spacing) — the plan's ordering assertions are exact, not approximate.
   532	
   533	---
   534

   542	- `fetchJson` rejection carries `{code, message, detail, status}` — assets/api.js:26-33, 37-78.
   543	- The sole innerHTML is markdown.js — assets/markdown.js:175-178; components.js header confirms — components.js:1-4.
   544	- Nav links already use `data-screen` — components.js:53.
   545	- `.button--primary` and accent chips carry the amber family — assets/app.css:239-243, 267-271, 320-324; tokens closed set — assets/tokens.css:7-58.
   546	- `_SHELL` current script list (insertion point) — src/planner/core/server.py:45-64.
   547	
   548	**Day + plan tree:**
   549	- `GET /api/day/{date}` (`today` resolved via planning date) → `{id, brief, notes, plan|null, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at, tickets:[ticket_json in position order]}` — src/planner/days/api.py:52-62, 65-80, 108-111.
   550	- `plan` dict shape `{root:{focus,status}, children:[{ticket_id,note,status,position}]}` — src/planner/days/logic/tree.py:26-42.
   551	- Plan routes accept/accept-all/invalidate/reject-all, human-only, child node must be an int — days/api.py:150-207, 83-91.
   552	- Accept-all accepts root+children and adds child tickets at the END if absent (idempotent) — tree.py:101-114; days/data.py:107-136 (position = current count).
   553	- `DELETE /api/day/{date}/tickets/{ticket_id}` = association-only delete + contiguous re-pack — days/api.py:140-147; days/data.py:139-162.
   554	- `POST /api/day/{date}/tickets` body `{ticket_id}` — days/api.py:129-137.
   555	
   556	**Queues + review:**
   557	- `GET /api/queues` → `{approvals, pickup, overdue}`; approvals entries `{entity_id, entity_type: "ticket"|"item", kind, title, waiting_since}` sorted oldest-first — src/planner/tickets/api.py:452-458; src/planner/tickets/views.py:265-325 (sort :310); kinds = gating field name | "review" | "status" — src/planner/days/logic/carryover.py:84-128; review `waiting_since` = last `state_changed→needs_review` event, else updated_at fallback — views.py:297-309.
   558	- Ticket detail: `state`, `ceiling`, `at_cap`, `fields.{success,approach,plan,result}` each `{value, proposal:{body,proposed_by,created_at}|null, notes}` — tickets/views.py:38-62, 121-160; contracts.py:63-83.
   559	- `POST /api/tickets/{id}/accept/{field}` body `{edited_body?, next_ceiling?, at_cap?}`, human-only — tickets/api.py:117-121, 304-322; grant pair mandatory (`grant_missing`), floor at-or-beyond the newly entered state, `"none"` sentinel pins to the new state (`grant_invalid` otherwise) — src/planner/tickets/logic/machine.py:75-100; wire sentinel `NO_FURTHER = "none"` — src/planner/tickets/contracts.py:86-87; STATE_ORDER — contracts.py:26-29; ADVANCE_TARGET + ceiling=done special case — contracts.py:55-60, machine.py:40-48.
   560	- Edit-accept: `edited` flag = `edited_body is not None`; stored verbatim; new state computed from the CURRENT ceiling; non-gating accepts need no grant — src/planner/tickets/logic/resolution.py:142-187.
   561	- `POST /api/tickets/{id}/approve` — human-only, no grant, needs_review→done — tickets/api.py:325-330; resolution.py:190-199.
   562	- `GET /api/items/{item_id}` → `status`, `status_proposal:{to_status,note,proposed_by,created_at}|null` — src/planner/sprints/api.py:202-204; src/planner/sprints/views.py:30-51.
   563	- `POST /api/items/{item_id}/accept-status` — human-only, no grant — sprints/api.py:244-248; `propose-status` — sprints/api.py:234-241.

   566

   569	- Day entity = `day_YYYY-MM-DD` (canonical check + materialize-on-read); session key persisted on first reply only; that's ALL the server stores about a conversation — src/planner/chat/service.py:37-61, 64-104.

   597	
   598	## 12. BINDING AMENDMENTS (post codex plan review — these override the sections above where they conflict; see plan-review.md for dispositions)
   599	
   600	**A1 — no `withAttr` helper.** §3's empty-queue line is built inline in screens-review.js:
   601	
   602	```js
   603	var empty = c.quietLine("nothing waiting");
   604	empty.setAttribute("data-review-empty", "");
   605	root.replaceChildren(empty);
   606	```
   607	
   608	No new exported helper; the §1.1 helper list is final (`quietLine`, `advanceTarget`, `gatingField`, `STATE_ORDER`).
   609	
   610	**A2 — `vanished` is DELETED.** screens-review.js keeps exactly ONE module-scope transient set: `skipped` (human skips + auto-skips; reload resets it; the wrap rule clears it when every entry is skipped). The two failure paths become:
   611	
   612	- **Detail-fetch rejection** (transient or otherwise): do NOT hide the entry and do NOT auto-refetch. Render an inline error surface in the screen root: `errorLine(err)`, a quiet line with `entry.title`, and a `button.button [data-skip] "Skip"` whose click does `skipped[key(entry)] = true; rerender();` — plus, for tickets, the `[data-open-ticket]` anchor. The human decides to move on; nothing is hidden for the session; no fetch loop is possible (nothing refetches until the human acts or a flush lands).
   613	- **Staleness guard** (detail fetched fine but the entry no longer matches: gating proposal null / gating field mismatch / state left needs_review / status_proposal null): if `skipped[key(entry)]` is NOT yet set — `skipped[key(entry)] = true; rerender();` (the fresh `/api/queues` in that rerender will almost always have dropped the entry). If it IS already set (i.e., the wrap rule re-surfaced an entry that is stale again — the only way a loop could start): render the A1 empty quiet line and stop. Bounded, transient, nothing permanently hidden — a reload or the next flush re-derives everything from the server.
   614	
   615	§8.4's mechanism text is superseded by this; its goal (no loop, no lost entries) stands.
   616	
   617	**A3 — stay disabled on success.** For every mutating control this ticket builds (proposalCard Accept, reviewCard Approve / Accept-status, planTree per-node Accept/Invalidate and Accept-all/Reject-all, the day row Remove, the chat Send is exempt — chat has no flush-replacement contract and already re-enables on settle by design §1.2): on click → disable (and clear any prior `.error-line`); on rejection → render `errorLine(err)` and re-enable (human corrects and retries); on success → **stay disabled** — the WS-flush re-render replaces the DOM, so re-enabling would only open the §8.8 double-fire gap. In proposalCard terms: `sync()` disables when `inFlight || resolved || (requireGrant && picker.getGrant() === null)`, where `resolved` latches true on fulfilled accept. §1.4's "finally inFlight = false" and §8.8's claim are superseded accordingly. T14's fieldEditor keeps its own settle-re-enable behavior — the foundation is not reworked.
   618	
   619	**A4 — innerHTML fence, corrected.** The self-check is: `grep -n 'innerHTML' assets/screens-day.js assets/screens-review.js` → no output, and the components.js additive block plus the app.css additions contain zero `innerHTML` occurrences (check the diff). No repo-wide count (markdown.js legitimately matches, comments included). §0.10 and §10.5 read accordingly.
   620	
   621	**A5 — smoke goto typo.** `page.goto(base + "/")` (T14 smoke.py:151 pattern). Applies wherever §7 navigates.
   622	
   623	**A6 — plan-node count selectors.** Smoke child-row assertions use `.plan-node--child` (expect 2 in the demo); the root row is `[data-node="root"]`; a bare `[data-node]` matches 3 and is only ever asserted as 3. §7(a)'s "exactly 2 `[data-node]` child rows" reads "exactly 2 `.plan-node--child` rows".

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/* Screen 2: Review (#/review). One-at-a-time approval walk, oldest-first from
     2	 * /api/queues approvals. Stateless render: each action calls fetchJson and relies
     3	 * on the WS flush -> route() re-render (the fresh queue drops resolved entries).
     4	 * The only client state is transient skip memory (module scope, per-session).
     5	 * Registers the real "review" screen over app.js's placeholder (overwrite-wins).
     6	 * Classic script: IIFE + "use strict", var/function style, createElement only. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = window.Planner;
    10	  var c = Planner.components;
    11	
    12	  // The one transient set (amendment A2): human skips + auto-skips. Reload resets
    13	  // it; the wrap rule clears it when every entry is skipped.
    14	  var skipped = {};
    15	
    16	  function key(entry) {
    17	    return entry.entity_id + ":" + entry.kind;
    18	  }
    19	
    20	  function render(root) {
    21	    root.setAttribute("data-screen", "review");
    22	    root.classList.add("review-screen");
    23	    Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/queues").then(function (q) {
    24	      var entries = q.approvals || [];   // oldest-first from the server (views.py:310)
    25	      if (!entries.length) {
    26	        showEmpty(root);
    27	        return;
    28	      }
    29	      var live = entries.filter(function (e) {
    30	        return !skipped[key(e)];
    31	      });
    32	      var wrapped = false;
    33	      if (!live.length) {
    34	        // Wrap rule: skipping the last entry starts over at the oldest.
    35	        skipped = {};
    36	        live = entries;
    37	        wrapped = true;
    38	      }
    39	      show(root, live[0], wrapped);
    40	    }, function (err) {
    41	      root.replaceChildren(c.errorLine(err));
    42	    });
    43	  }
    44	
    45	  function showEmpty(root) {
    46	    var empty = c.quietLine("nothing waiting");
    47	    empty.setAttribute("data-review-empty", "");
    48	    root.replaceChildren(empty);
    49	  }
    50	
    51	  function isStale(entry, detail) {
    52	    var kind = entry.kind;
    53	    if (kind === "review") {
    54	      return detail.state !== "needs_review";
    55	    }
    56	    if (kind === "status") {
    57	      return !detail.status_proposal;
    58	    }
    59	    // gating kinds: proposal must still be pending on the current gating field.
    60	    if (!detail.fields || !detail.fields[kind] || !detail.fields[kind].proposal) {
    61	      return true;
    62	    }
    63	    return c.gatingField(detail.state) !== kind;
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  function detailError(root, entry, err) {
    67	    // A2: detail-fetch rejection. Do NOT hide the entry and do NOT auto-refetch.
    68	    // Surface an inline error; the human decides to move on via Skip.
    69	    var wrap = document.createElement("div");
    70	    wrap.appendChild(c.errorLine(err));
    71	    wrap.appendChild(c.quietLine(entry.title));
    72	    var footer = document.createElement("div");
    73	    footer.className = "review-card-actions";
    74	    var skip = document.createElement("button");
    75	    skip.type = "button";
    76	    skip.className = "button";
    77	    skip.setAttribute("data-skip", "");
    78	    skip.textContent = "Skip";
    79	    skip.addEventListener("click", function () {
    80	      skipped[key(entry)] = true;
    81	      render(root);
    82	    });
    83	    footer.appendChild(skip);
    84	    if (entry.entity_type === "ticket") {
    85	      var open = document.createElement("a");
    86	      open.setAttribute("data-open-ticket", "");
    87	      open.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + entry.entity_id);
    88	      open.textContent = "open ticket";
    89	      footer.appendChild(open);
    90	    }
    91	    wrap.appendChild(footer);
    92	    root.replaceChildren(wrap);
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  function show(root, entry, wrapped) {
    96	    var path = entry.entity_type === "ticket" ? "/api/tickets/" : "/api/items/";
    97	    Planner.api.fetchJson(path + entry.entity_id).then(function (detail) {
    98	      if (isStale(entry, detail)) {
    99	        // A2 staleness guard: skip once and re-derive; if a wrap re-surfaced an
   100	        // entry that is stale again, stop at the empty line (bounded, no loop).
   101	        if (skipped[key(entry)] || wrapped) {
   102	          showEmpty(root);
   103	        } else {
   104	          skipped[key(entry)] = true;
   105	          render(root);
   106	        }
   107	        return;
   108	      }
   109	      root.replaceChildren(c.reviewCard({
   110	        entry: entry,
   111	        detail: detail,
   112	        onSkip: function () {
   113	          skipped[key(entry)] = true;
   114	          render(root);
   115	        },
   116	        onAccept: function (payload) {
   117	          return Planner.api.fetchJson(
   118	            "/api/tickets/" + entry.entity_id + "/accept/" + entry.kind,
   119	            { method: "POST", body: payload }
   120	          );
   121	        },
   122	        onApprove: function () {
   123	          return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + entry.entity_id + "/approve", {
   124	            method: "POST",
   125	            body: {}
   126	          });
   127	        },
   128	        onAcceptStatus: function () {
   129	          return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/items/" + entry.entity_id + "/accept-status", {
   130	            method: "POST",
   131	            body: {}
   132	          });
   133	        }
   134	      }));
   135	    }, function (err) {
   136	      detailError(root, entry, err);
   137	    });
   138	  }
   139	
   140	  Planner.registerScreen("review", render);
   141	})();

     9	  function make(tag, className, text) {
    10	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    11	    if (className) {
    12	      node.className = className;
    13	    }
    14	    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) {
    15	      node.textContent = text;
    16	    }

    18	  }

    21	    if (children === null || children === undefined) {
    22	      return;
    23	    }
    24	    if (Array.isArray(children)) {
    25	      children.forEach(function (child) {
    26	        if (child) {
    27	          parent.appendChild(child);
    28	        }
    29	      });
    30	    } else {
    31	      parent.appendChild(children);
    32	    }

   188	    return anchor;
   189	  }
   190	
   191	  // 5.7 Error line — consumes exactly what fetchJson rejections carry.
   192	  function errorLine(err) {
   193	    var line = make("div", "error-line");
   194	    line.appendChild(make("span", "error-code", (err && err.code) || "error"));
   195	    line.appendChild(make("span", "error-message", (err && err.message) || "request failed"));
   196	    return line;
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  // 5.8 Create form (D11 #13) — item / idea variants. Owns form mechanics ONLY
   200	  // (disable, clear, error placement); the screen's onSubmit builds the request
   201	  // body and returns the fetchJson promise. No optimistic UI — a successful create
   202	  // is reflected only when the WS-invalidation refetch re-renders the list.
   203	  var PROJECTS = ["Vylo", "Tribe", "Learning", "Other"];   // §3.2 Project enum
   204	  var PRIORITIES = ["P0", "P1", "P2", "P3"];                // §3.2 priorities
   205	  var FORM_SPECS = {
   206	    item: {
   207	      submitLabel: "Add item",
   208	      fields: [
   209	        { name: "title", kind: "text", label: "Title", required: true, placeholder: "Item title" },
   210	        { name: "project", kind: "select", label: "Project", options: PROJECTS, default: "Vylo" },
   211	        { name: "priority", kind: "select", label: "Priority", options: PRIORITIES, default: "P3" },
   212	        { name: "deadline", kind: "date", label: "Deadline" }
   213	      ]
   214	    },
   215	    idea: {
   216	      submitLabel: "Add idea",
   217	      fields: [
   218	        { name: "title", kind: "text", label: "Title", required: true, placeholder: "Idea title" },
   219	        { name: "project", kind: "select", label: "Project", options: PROJECTS,
   220	          includeBlank: true, blankLabel: "— no project —", default: "" },
   221	        { name: "body", kind: "textarea", label: "Body" }
   222	      ]
   223	    }
   224	  };
   225	
   226	  function createForm(variant, onSubmit) {
   227	    var spec = FORM_SPECS[variant];
   228	    var form = make("form", "create-form");
   229	    form.setAttribute("data-create", variant);
   230	    var controls = [];
   231	    spec.fields.forEach(function (field) {
   232	      var wrap = make("div", "create-form-field");
   233	      wrap.appendChild(make("label", "create-form-label", field.label));
   234	      var control;
   235	      var reset = "";
   236	      if (field.kind === "select") {
   237	        control = make("select", "form-control");
   238	        if (field.includeBlank) {
   239	          var blank = make("option", null, field.blankLabel);
   240	          blank.value = "";
   241	          control.appendChild(blank);
   242	        }
   243	        field.options.forEach(function (opt) {
   244	          var option = make("option", null, opt);
   245	          option.value = opt;
   246	          control.appendChild(option);
   247	        });
   248	        reset = field.default !== undefined
   249	          ? field.default
   250	          : (field.includeBlank ? "" : field.options[0]);
   251	        control.value = reset;
   252	      } else if (field.kind === "textarea") {
   253	        control = make("textarea", "form-control");
   254	        control.rows = 4;
   255	      } else {
   256	        control = make("input", "form-control");
   257	        control.type = field.kind === "date" ? "date" : "text";
   258	        if (field.placeholder) {
   259	          control.setAttribute("placeholder", field.placeholder);
   260	        }
   261	      }
   262	      control.setAttribute("data-input", field.name);
   263	      if (field.required) {
   264	        control.required = true;
   265	      }
   266	      wrap.appendChild(control);
   267	      form.appendChild(wrap);
   268	      controls.push({ name: field.name, control: control, reset: reset });
   269	    });
   270	    var actions = make("div", "field-editor-actions");
   271	    var submit = make("button", "button button--primary", spec.submitLabel);
   272	    submit.type = "submit";
   273	    actions.appendChild(submit);
   274	    form.appendChild(actions);
   275	
   276	    form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
   277	      e.preventDefault();
   278	      var prior = form.querySelector(".error-line");
   279	      if (prior) {
   280	        prior.parentNode.removeChild(prior);
   281	      }
   282	      if (!form.checkValidity()) {
   283	        form.reportValidity();
   284	        return;
   285	      }
   286	      var values = {};
   287	      controls.forEach(function (entry) {
   288	        values[entry.name] = entry.control.value;
   289	      });
   290	      submit.disabled = true;
   291	      Promise.resolve(onSubmit(values)).then(
   292	        function () {
   293	          controls.forEach(function (entry) {
   294	            entry.control.value = entry.reset;
   295	          });
   296	        },
   297	        function (err) {
   298	          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
   299	        }
   300	      ).then(function () {
   301	        submit.disabled = false;
   302	      });
   303	    });
   304	    return form;
   305	  }

   308	  // T15 additions (D11 items 7, 8, 9, 10, 16) — Day + Review composites, the

   342	  function advanceTarget(state, ceiling) {

   346	    }

   358	  // Shared mutating-button discipline (amendment A3): on click clear any prior
   359	  // error and disable; on rejection re-enable and surface the error; on SUCCESS
   360	  // stay disabled — the WS-flush re-render replaces the DOM, and re-enabling would
   361	  // only reopen the flush-gap double-fire window. Private helper (not exported).
   362	  function bindMutating(button, container, run) {
   363	    button.addEventListener("click", function () {
   364	      var prior = container.querySelector(".error-line");
   365	      if (prior) {
   366	        container.removeChild(prior);
   367	      }
   368	      button.disabled = true;
   369	      Promise.resolve(run()).then(
   370	        function () {},
   371	        function (err) {
   372	          button.disabled = false;
   373	          container.prepend(errorLine(err));
   374	        }
   375	      );
   376	    });
   377	  }

   379	  // --- the pluggable chat-input source (SPEC §14 audio seam) -----------------
   380	  // A source is factory(ctx) -> HTMLElement. ctx.submit(text) -> Promise is the
   381	  // only way a source delivers input (the panel owns transport); ctx.initialText
   382	  // is the preserved draft; ctx.onInput(text) reports pending text so the panel
   383	  // can preserve the draft across re-renders.
   384	  function makeTextInputSource(ctx) {
   385	    var wrap = make("div", "chat-input");
   386	    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input chat-input-text");
   387	    textarea.setAttribute("data-chat-input", "");
   388	    textarea.rows = 2;
   389	    textarea.value = ctx.initialText || "";
   390	    textarea.addEventListener("input", function () {
   391	      ctx.onInput(textarea.value);
   392	    });
   393	    var send = make("button", "button button--primary", "Send");
   394	    send.type = "button";
   395	    send.setAttribute("data-chat-send", "");
   396	    send.addEventListener("click", function () {
   397	      var text = textarea.value.trim();
   398	      if (!text) {
   399	        return;
   400	      }
   401	      send.disabled = true;
   402	      textarea.value = "";
   403	      ctx.onInput("");
   404	      Promise.resolve(ctx.submit(text)).then(
   405	        function () {
   406	          send.disabled = false;
   407	        },
   408	        function () {
   409	          send.disabled = false;
   410	        }
   411	      );
   412	    });
   413	    wrap.appendChild(textarea);
   414	    wrap.appendChild(send);
   415	    return wrap;
   416	  }

   508	    function sync() {
   509	      accept.disabled = inFlight || resolved ||
   510	        (opts.requireGrant && (picker === null || picker.getGrant() === null));
   511	    }
   512	
   513	    if (opts.requireGrant) {
   514	      picker = grantPairPicker(opts.newState, sync);
   515	      card.appendChild(picker);
   516	    }
   517	
   518	    accept.addEventListener("click", function () {
   519	      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");
   520	      if (prior) {
   521	        actions.removeChild(prior);
   522	      }
   523	      var payload = {};
   524	      if (textarea.value !== opts.proposal.body) {
   525	        payload.edited_body = textarea.value;
   526	      }
   527	      if (opts.requireGrant) {
   528	        var grant = picker.getGrant();
   529	        payload.next_ceiling = grant.next_ceiling;
   530	        payload.at_cap = grant.at_cap;
   531	      }
   532	      inFlight = true;
   533	      sync();
   534	      Promise.resolve(opts.onAccept(payload)).then(
   535	        function () {
   536	          resolved = true;   // A3: stay disabled after a successful accept
   537	          inFlight = false;
   538	          sync();
   539	        },
   540	        function (err) {
   541	          inFlight = false;
   542	          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
   543	          sync();
   544	        }
   545	      );
   546	    });
   547	
   548	    actions.appendChild(accept);
   549	    card.appendChild(actions);
   550	    sync();   // Accept starts disabled when a grant is required (nothing picked)
   551	    return card;
   552	  }
   553	
   554	  // --- D11 item 9: day-plan tree ---------------------------------------------
   555	  // node is "root" (string) or the child position (NUMBER — the server rejects
   556	  // string positions, days/api.py:83-91). No local status mutation: the four
   557	  // routes append events, so the WS flush re-render shows the new tree.
   558	  function planTree(plan, handlers) {
   559	    var wrap = make("div", "plan-tree");
   560	    var actions = make("div", "plan-tree-actions");
   561	
   562	    var acceptAll = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept all");
   563	    acceptAll.type = "button";
   564	    acceptAll.setAttribute("data-accept-all", "");
   565	    bindMutating(acceptAll, actions, function () {
   566	      return handlers.acceptAll();
   567	    });
   568	    var rejectAll = make("button", "button", "Reject all");
   569	    rejectAll.type = "button";
   570	    rejectAll.setAttribute("data-reject-all", "");
   571	    bindMutating(rejectAll, actions, function () {
   572	      return handlers.rejectAll();
   573	    });
   574	    actions.appendChild(acceptAll);
   575	    actions.appendChild(rejectAll);
   576	    wrap.appendChild(actions);
   577	
   578	    function makeNode(ref, isRoot, label, status, ticketId) {
   579	      var node = make("div", isRoot ? "plan-node plan-node--root" : "plan-node plan-node--child");
   580	      node.setAttribute("data-node", isRoot ? "root" : String(ref));
   581	      node.setAttribute("data-status", status);
   582	      node.appendChild(make("span", "plan-node-status", status));
   583	      node.appendChild(make("span", isRoot ? "plan-node-focus" : "plan-node-note", label));
   584	      if (!isRoot && ticketId) {
   585	        var link = make("a", "plan-node-ticket", "open");
   586	        link.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + ticketId);
   587	        node.appendChild(link);
   588	      }
   589	      var nodeActions = make("span", "plan-node-actions");
   590	      if (status !== "accepted") {   // Accept only offered on a not-yet-accepted node
   591	        var acceptNode = make("button", "button", "Accept");
   592	        acceptNode.type = "button";
   593	        acceptNode.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
   594	        bindMutating(acceptNode, actions, function () {
   595	          return handlers.accept(ref);
   596	        });
   597	        nodeActions.appendChild(acceptNode);
   598	      }
   599	      var invalidate = make("button", "button", "Invalidate");
   600	      invalidate.type = "button";
   601	      invalidate.setAttribute("data-invalidate", "");
   602	      bindMutating(invalidate, actions, function () {
   603	        return handlers.invalidate(ref);
   604	      });
   605	      nodeActions.appendChild(invalidate);
   606	      node.appendChild(nodeActions);
   607	      return node;
   608	    }
   609	
   610	    wrap.appendChild(makeNode("root", true, plan.root.focus, plan.root.status, null));
   611	    (plan.children || []).forEach(function (child) {
   612	      wrap.appendChild(
   613	        makeNode(child.position, false, child.note || child.ticket_id, child.status, child.ticket_id)
   614	      );
   615	    });
   616	    return wrap;
   617	  }
   618	
   619	  // --- D11 item 10: chat panel -----------------------------------------------
   620	  // entityId is the SERVER-provided chat id (day.id / ticket.id), never built
   621	  // client-side. Rebuilds its message list from the module-scope transcript, which
   622	  // the WS flush does not touch — that is the survival mechanism.
   623	  function chatPanel(entityId, opts) {
   624	    var panelEl = make("div", "chat-panel");
   625	    panelEl.setAttribute("data-chat-panel", "");
   626	    var messages = make("div", "chat-messages");
   627	    messages.setAttribute("data-chat-messages", "");
   628	    panelEl.appendChild(messages);
   629	
   630	    function paint() {
   631	      messages.replaceChildren();
   632	      (chatTranscripts[entityId] || []).forEach(function (msg) {
   633	        if (msg.who === "you") {
   634	          var you = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--you", msg.text);
   635	          you.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "you");
   636	          messages.appendChild(you);
   637	        } else {
   638	          var reply = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--planner");
   639	          reply.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "planner");
   640	          reply.appendChild(markdownBlock(msg.text));
   641	          messages.appendChild(reply);
   642	        }
   643	      });
   644	    }
   645	    paint();
   646	
   647	    if (!opts.available) {
   648	      var offline = make("div", "chat-offline", "gateway offline");
   649	      offline.setAttribute("data-chat-offline", "");
   650	      panelEl.appendChild(offline);
   651	      return panelEl;   // §11 notice; no input source rendered when offline
   652	    }
   653	
   654	    var source;
   655	    var ctx = {
   656	      initialText: chatDrafts[entityId] || "",
   657	      onInput: function (text) {
   658	        chatDrafts[entityId] = text;
   659	      },
   660	      submit: function (text) {
   661	        if (!chatTranscripts[entityId]) {
   662	          chatTranscripts[entityId] = [];
   663	        }
   664	        chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "you", text: text });
   665	        delete chatDrafts[entityId];
   666	        paint();
   667	        return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + entityId + "/send", {
   668	          method: "POST",
   669	          body: { text: text }
   670	        }).then(
   671	          function (res) {
   672	            chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "planner", text: res.reply_text });
   673	            if (panelEl.isConnected) {   // a flush may have replaced the panel mid-flight
   674	              paint();
   675	            }
   676	            return res;
   677	          },
   678	          function (err) {
   679	            var prior = panelEl.querySelector(".error-line");
   680	            if (prior) {
   681	              panelEl.removeChild(prior);
   682	            }
   683	            panelEl.insertBefore(errorLine(err), source);
   684	            return Promise.reject(err);
   685	          }
   686	        );
   687	      }
   688	    };
   689	    source = Planner.chatInput.sources[Planner.chatInput.active](ctx);
   690	    panelEl.appendChild(source);
   691	    return panelEl;
   692	  }
   693	
   694	  // --- D11 item 16: review card ----------------------------------------------
   695	  // The shell is panel() (D11 #2 — the only box primitive). The screen guards
   696	  // staleness before constructing (proposal null / state moved on).
   697	  function reviewCard(opts) {
   698	    var entry = opts.entry;
   699	    var detail = opts.detail;
   700	    var kind = entry.kind;
   701	    var gating = kind === "success" || kind === "approach" ||
   702	      kind === "plan" || kind === "result";
   703	    var content = [];
   704	    var head = make("div", "review-card-head");
   705	    var footer = make("div", "review-card-actions");
   706	    var primary = null;
   707	
   708	    if (gating) {
   709	      head.appendChild(chip("state", detail.state));
   710	      head.appendChild(chip("pending-proposal"));
   711	      content.push(head);
   712	      content.push(proposalCard({
   713	        proposal: detail.fields[kind].proposal,
   714	        requireGrant: true,
   715	        newState: advanceTarget(detail.state, detail.ceiling),
   716	        onAccept: opts.onAccept
   717	      }));
   718	    } else if (kind === "review") {
   719	      head.appendChild(chip("state", detail.state));
   720	      content.push(head);
   721	      content.push(markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.value));
   722	      if (detail.fields.result.notes) {
   723	        var note = make("div", "review-note");
   724	        note.appendChild(markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.notes));
   725	        content.push(note);
   726	      }
   727	      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Approve");
   728	      primary.type = "button";
   729	      primary.setAttribute("data-approve", "");
   730	      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
   731	        return opts.onApprove();
   732	      });
   733	    } else if (kind === "status") {
   734	      head.appendChild(chip(null, detail.status));
   735	      head.appendChild(chip(null, "→ " + detail.status_proposal.to_status));
   736	      content.push(head);
   737	      if (detail.status_proposal.note) {
   738	        content.push(make("div", "review-note", detail.status_proposal.note));
   739	      }
   740	      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
   741	      primary.type = "button";
   742	      primary.setAttribute("data-accept-status", "");
   743	      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
   744	        return opts.onAcceptStatus();
   745	      });
   746	    }
   747	
   748	    if (primary) {
   749	      footer.appendChild(primary);
   750	    }
   751	    var skip = make("button", "button", "Skip");
   752	    skip.type = "button";
   753	    skip.setAttribute("data-skip", "");
   754	    skip.addEventListener("click", function () {
   755	      opts.onSkip();
   756	    });
   757	    footer.appendChild(skip);
   758	    if (entry.entity_type === "ticket") {
   759	      var open = make("a", "review-open", "open ticket");
   760	      open.setAttribute("data-open-ticket", "");
   761	      open.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + entry.entity_id);
   762	      footer.appendChild(open);
   763	    }
   764	    content.push(footer);
   765	
   766	    var section = panel(entry.title, content);
   767	    section.setAttribute("data-review-card", "");
   768	    section.setAttribute("data-entity-id", entry.entity_id);
   769	    section.setAttribute("data-kind", kind);
   770	    if (gating) {
   771	      section.setAttribute("data-field", kind);
   772	    }
   773	    return section;
   774	  }
   775	
   776	  Planner.components = {
   777	    appShell: appShell,
   778	    panel: panel,
   779	    markdownBlock: markdownBlock,
   780	    fieldEditor: fieldEditor,
   781	    chip: chip,
   782	    entityRow: entityRow,
   783	    errorLine: errorLine,
   784	    createForm: createForm,
   785	    quietLine: quietLine,
   786	    advanceTarget: advanceTarget,
   787	    gatingField: gatingField,
   788	    STATE_ORDER: STATE_ORDER,
   789	    proposalCard: proposalCard,
   790	    grantPairPicker: grantPairPicker,
   791	    planTree: planTree,
   792	    chatPanel: chatPanel,
   793	    reviewCard: reviewCard
   794	  };
   795	
   796	  // The pluggable chat-input registry (SPEC §14 audio seam). A later audio script
   797	  // registers a second source with one <script> tag and no edits here: chatPanel
   798	  // reads sources[active] at render time and couples only to the ctx contract.
   799	  Planner.chatInput = {
   800	    sources: { text: makeTextInputSource },
   801	    active: "text",
   802	    register: function (name, factory, makeActive) {
   803	      Planner.chatInput.sources[name] = factory;
   804	      if (makeActive) {
   805	        Planner.chatInput.active = name;
   806	      }
   807	    }
   808	  };
   809	})();

    69	def http_status_for(code: ErrorCode) -> int:
    70	    return _STATUS_BY_CODE.get(code, 400)

    73	def create_app(
    74	    config: Config,
    75	    clock: Clock,
    76	    adapters: Adapters,
    77	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    78	) -> FastAPI:
    79	    # The DDL carries the literal 200 title cap; the config value is what write
    80	    # paths enforce. Guard that they agree (amendment 11). Factory time is startup
    81	    # and strictly earlier than any request, so this fires even for TestClient apps
    82	    # that never enter the lifespan.
    83	    assert config.title_max_chars == 200, "title_max_chars must equal the DDL literal (200)"
    84	
    85	    @asynccontextmanager
    86	    async def _lifespan(app_: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    87	        Path(config.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    88	        Path(config.logs_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    89	        loops: Any = None
    90	        if not config.test_mode:  # D6: background loops never run in test mode
    91	            try:
    92	                module = importlib.import_module("planner.core.loops")
    93	                start = module.start_background_loops
    94	            except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    95	                _log.warning("planner.core.loops unavailable; running without background loops")
    96	            else:
    97	                loops = start(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
    98	        try:
    99	            yield
   100	        finally:
   101	            if loops is not None:
   102	                await loops.stop()
   103	
   104	    app = FastAPI(title="planner", version="2.0.0", lifespan=_lifespan)
   105	    app.state.config = config
   106	    app.state.clock = clock
   107	    app.state.adapters = adapters
   108	    app.state.conn_factory = conn_factory
   109	
   110	    @app.exception_handler(PlannerError)
   111	    async def handle_planner_error(request: Request, exc: PlannerError) -> JSONResponse:
   112	        return JSONResponse(status_code=http_status_for(exc.code), content=exc.to_payload())
   113	
   114	    for domain_router in (
   115	        tickets_router,
   116	        sprints_router,
   117	        days_router,
   118	        dispatch_router,
   119	        seed_router,
   120	        chat_router,

    53	def free_or(preferred: int) -> int:
    54	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as probe:
    55	        try:
    56	            probe.bind(("127.0.0.1", preferred))
    57	            return preferred
    58	        except OSError:
    59	            pass
    60	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as fallback:
    61	        fallback.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    62	        return int(fallback.getsockname()[1])
    63

    65	def wait_ready(base: str, budget: float = 15.0, interval: float = 0.25) -> None:
    66	    deadline = time.time() + budget
    67	    while time.time() < deadline:
    68	        try:
    69	            if httpx.get(f"{base}/api/meta", timeout=1.0).status_code == 200:
    70	                return
    71	        except httpx.HTTPError:
    72	            pass
    73	        time.sleep(interval)
    74	    raise AssertionError("server did not become ready within budget")
    75	
    76	
    77	def start_server(tmp: Path, today: str, gateway: str | None = None) -> dict:
    78	    """Boot one seeded, normalized, in-process server on a daemon thread. The seed
    79	    stamps real wall time while the clock is frozen at fake noon (demo.py:29-30 vs
    80	    clock.py:27-41), so ticket timestamps are normalized to noon-600 — that keeps
    81	    t5's review entry (updated_at fallback) the oldest approval (views.py:297-309)."""
    82	    port = free_or(PORT)
    83	    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    84	    env = {
    85	        "PLAN_TEST_MODE": "1",
    86	        "PLAN_FAKE_NOW": today + "T12:00:00",
    87	        "PLAN_DB_PATH": str(tmp / "planning.db"),
    88	        "PLAN_PORT": str(port),
    89	        "PLAN_LOGS_DIR": str(tmp / "logs"),
    90	        "PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH": str(tmp / "dispatcher.lock"),
    91	        "PLAN_WS_POLL_MS": "50",  # tighten batch cadence; ui_debounce_ms stays 250
    92	    }
    93	    if gateway is not None:
    94	        env["PLAN_GATEWAY_ADAPTER"] = gateway
    95	    config = load_config(path=None, env=env)
    96	
    97	    (tmp / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    98	    with connect(config.db_path, config.db_busy_timeout_ms) as bootstrap:
    99	        create_schema(bootstrap)
   100	    seed = connect(config.db_path, config.db_busy_timeout_ms)
   101	    try:
   102	        seed_demo(seed)
   103	        anchor = int(datetime.fromisoformat(today + "T12:00:00").astimezone().timestamp()) - 600
   104	        seed.execute("UPDATE tickets SET created_at = ?, updated_at = ?", (anchor, anchor))
   105	        seed.commit()
   106	    finally:
   107	        seed.close()
   108	
   109	    clock = build_clock(config)
   110	    adapters = build_adapters(config)
   111	
   112	    def conn_factory():
   113	        return connect(config.db_path, config.db_busy_timeout_ms)
   114	
   115	    app = create_app(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
   116	    server = uvicorn.Server(
   117	        uvicorn.Config(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=port, log_level="warning")
   118	    )
   119	    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.run, daemon=True)
   120	    thread.start()
   121	    wait_ready(base)
   122	    return {"server": server, "thread": thread, "base": base}

   124	
   125	def attr_list(page, selector: str, attr: str) -> list:
   126	    return page.eval_on_selector_all(
   127	        selector, f"els => els.map(e => e.getAttribute('{attr}'))"
   128	    )
   129	
   130	
   131	def ticket_ids(page) -> list:
   132	    return attr_list(page, "[data-ticket-id]", "data-ticket-id")
   133	
   134	
   135	def wait_row_count(page, count: int) -> None:
   136	    page.wait_for_function(
   137	        "n => document.querySelectorAll('[data-ticket-id]').length === n",
   138	        arg=count,
   139	        timeout=5000,
   140	    )
   141	
   142	
   143	def wait_enabled(page, selector: str) -> None:
   144	    page.wait_for_function(
   145	        "sel => { var b = document.querySelector(sel); return !!b && !b.disabled; }",
   146	        arg=selector,
   147	        timeout=5000,
   148	    )
   149	
   150

     1	/* Hash router, screen registry, and boot. Owns the shell instance. The router
     2	 * exposes exactly Planner.registerScreen(name, render) — screens navigate via
     3	 * anchors and read params; no navigate(), no route events, no current-route
     4	 * getter. Every render derives from location.hash plus fresh fetches, so refresh
     5	 * restores state by construction. Classic script: attaches Planner.registerScreen. */
     6	(function () {
     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
     9	  var config = Planner.config;
    10	
    11	  var registry = {};
    12	  var shell = null;
    13	  var lastHash = null;
    14	
    15	  Planner.registerScreen = function (name, render) {
    16	    registry[name] = { render: render };
    17	  };
    18	
    19	  function quietLine(text) {
    20	    var div = document.createElement("div");
    21	    div.className = "quiet-line";
    22	    div.textContent = text;
    23	    return div;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  // Placeholders — T15–T17 scripts, appended after this file, re-register the real
    27	  // screens over these before DOMContentLoaded fires (overwrite-wins).
    28	  ["day", "review", "board", "ticket", "sprint", "backlog"].forEach(function (name) {
    29	    Planner.registerScreen(name, function (root) {
    30	      root.appendChild(quietLine("not built yet"));
    31	    });
    32	  });
    33	
    34	  function route() {
    35	    var hash = location.hash;
    36	    if (hash === "" || hash === "#") {
    37	      location.replace(config.ROUTES.day);
    38	      return;
    39	    }
    40	    var segments = hash.slice(1).split("/").filter(Boolean);
    41	    var name = segments[0];
    42	    var params = {};
    43	    if (name === "ticket") {
    44	      params.id = segments[1];
    45	    }
    46	    var known = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, name);
    47	    // Exact route shapes only: #/<screen> or #/ticket/<id> — no trailing extras.
    48	    var badShape = name === "ticket"
    49	      ? segments.length !== 2 || !params.id
    50	      : segments.length !== 1;
    51	    if (!known || badShape) {
    52	      shell.setActiveNav(null);
    53	      shell.content.replaceChildren(quietLine("no such screen"));
    54	      return;
    55	    }
    56	    shell.setActiveNav(name);
    57	    var entrance = hash !== lastHash;
    58	    lastHash = hash;
    59	    var screenDiv = document.createElement("div");
    60	    screenDiv.className = entrance ? "screen screen-enter" : "screen";
    61	    shell.content.replaceChildren(screenDiv);
    62	    registry[name].render(screenDiv, params);
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  function refreshBadge() {
    66	    Planner.api.fetchJson(config.API.QUEUES).then(
    67	      function (res) {
    68	        shell.setReviewBadge((res.approvals || []).length);
    69	      },
    70	      function () {
    71	        console.debug("[planner] queues fetch failed; badge unchanged");
    72	      }
    73	    );
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  function onFlush() {
    77	    refreshBadge();
    78	    route();
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  function boot() {
    82	    shell = Planner.components.appShell();
    83	    document.getElementById("app").appendChild(shell);
    84	
    85	    Planner.api.fetchJson(config.API.META).then(
    86	      function (meta) {
    87	        return meta.ui_debounce_ms;
    88	      },
    89	      function () {
    90	        console.debug("[planner] meta fetch failed; using default debounce");
    91	        return config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    92	      }
    93	    ).then(function (debounceMs) {
    94	      Planner.bus.onInvalidate(onFlush);
    95	      Planner.bus.start(debounceMs);
    96	      window.addEventListener("hashchange", route);
    97	      refreshBadge();
    98	      route();
    99	    });
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
   103	    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
   104	  } else {
   105	    boot();
   106	  }
   107	})();

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba assets/api.js | sed -n '1,120p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/* Data layer: JSON fetch wrapper (structured-error passthrough), the WS client,
     2	 * and the invalidation bus (SPEC §9). No client-side state store — the bus holds
     3	 * only a cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff delay, and one flush callback; every
     4	 * flush makes the active screen refetch. WS batches are an invalidation signal:
     5	 * onmessage reads only msg.cursor, never the events array.
     6	 * Classic script: attaches Planner.api and Planner.bus; defines window.__plannerDebug. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
    10	  var config = Planner.config;
    11	
    12	  // The sanctioned console hook: counters only, one console.debug per flush / open.
    13	  window.__plannerDebug = { flushes: 0, wsOpens: 0, cursor: 0 };
    14	
    15	  function makeError(code, message, extra) {
    16	    var err = new Error(message);
    17	    err.code = code;
    18	    if (extra) {
    19	      Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (key) {
    20	        err[key] = extra[key];
    21	      });
    22	    }
    23	    return err;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  function makePlannerError(envelope, status) {
    27	    var err = new Error(envelope.message);
    28	    err.code = envelope.code;
    29	    err.detail = envelope.detail;
    30	    err.status = status;
    31	    err.isPlannerError = true;
    32	    return err;
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  // --- fetchJson ---------------------------------------------------------------
    36	
    37	  function fetchJson(path, options) {
    38	    options = options || {};
    39	    var init = { method: options.method || "GET", headers: {} };
    40	    if (options.body !== undefined && options.body !== null) {
    41	      init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    42	      init.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    43	    }
    44	    return fetch(path, init).then(
    45	      function (response) {
    46	        return response.text().then(function (raw) {
    47	          return { response: response, raw: raw };
    48	        });
    49	      },
    50	      function () {
    51	        return Promise.reject(makeError("network", "network error"));
    52	      }
    53	    ).then(function (pair) {
    54	      var response = pair.response;
    55	      var raw = pair.raw;
    56	      if (response.ok) {
    57	        try {
    58	          return raw === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(raw);
    59	        } catch (parseErr) {
    60	          return Promise.reject(
    61	            makeError("bad_json", "invalid JSON in response", { status: response.status })
    62	          );
    63	        }
    64	      }
    65	      var parsed = null;
    66	      try {
    67	        parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    68	      } catch (parseErr) {
    69	        parsed = null;
    70	      }
    71	      if (parsed && parsed.error && parsed.error.code) {
    72	        return Promise.reject(makePlannerError(parsed.error, response.status));
    73	      }
    74	      return Promise.reject(
    75	        makeError("http_error", "HTTP " + response.status, { status: response.status })
    76	      );
    77	    });
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  Planner.api = { fetchJson: fetchJson };
    81	
    82	  // --- the invalidation bus ----------------------------------------------------
    83	
    84	  var cursor = 0;
    85	  var socket = null;
    86	  var debounceMs = config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    87	  var flushTimer = null;
    88	  var retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
    89	  var onFlush = null;
    90	  var started = false;
    91	
    92	  function flush() {
    93	    flushTimer = null;
    94	    window.__plannerDebug.flushes += 1;
    95	    console.debug("[planner] flush " + window.__plannerDebug.flushes);
    96	    if (onFlush) {
    97	      onFlush();
    98	    }
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  function schedule() {
   102	    clearTimeout(flushTimer);
   103	    flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, debounceMs);
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  function connect() {
   107	    var scheme = location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
   108	    var url = scheme + location.host + config.API.EVENTS_WS + "?since=" + cursor;
   109	    socket = new WebSocket(url);
   110	    socket.onopen = function () {
   111	      retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
   112	      window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens += 1;
   113	      console.debug("[planner] ws open since=" + cursor);
   114	    };
   115	    socket.onmessage = function (event) {
   116	      var msg;
   117	      try {
   118	        msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
   119	      } catch (parseErr) {
   120	        console.debug("[planner] ws bad json");

+  // 5.8 Create form (D11 #13) — item / idea variants. Owns form mechanics ONLY
+  // (disable, clear, error placement); the screen's onSubmit builds the request
+  // body and returns the fetchJson promise. No optimistic UI — a successful create

+  var PROJECTS = ["Vylo", "Tribe", "Learning", "Other"];   // §3.2 Project enum
+  var PRIORITIES = ["P0", "P1", "P2", "P3"];                // §3.2 priorities
+  var FORM_SPECS = {
+    item: {
+      submitLabel: "Add item",
+      fields: [
+        { name: "title", kind: "text", label: "Title", required: true, placeholder: "Item title" },
+        { name: "project", kind: "select", label: "Project", options: PROJECTS, default: "Vylo" },
+        { name: "priority", kind: "select", label: "Priority", options: PRIORITIES, default: "P3" },
+        { name: "deadline", kind: "date", label: "Deadline" }
+      ]

+    idea: {
+      submitLabel: "Add idea",
+      fields: [
+        { name: "title", kind: "text", label: "Title", required: true, placeholder: "Idea title" },
+        { name: "project", kind: "select", label: "Project", options: PROJECTS,
+          includeBlank: true, blankLabel: "— no project —", default: "" },
+        { name: "body", kind: "textarea", label: "Body" }
+      ]
+    }
+  };
+
+  function createForm(variant, onSubmit) {
+    var spec = FORM_SPECS[variant];
+    var form = make("form", "create-form");
+    form.setAttribute("data-create", variant);
+    var controls = [];
+    spec.fields.forEach(function (field) {
+      var wrap = make("div", "create-form-field");
+      wrap.appendChild(make("label", "create-form-label", field.label));
+      var control;
+      var reset = "";
+      if (field.kind === "select") {
+        control = make("select", "form-control");
+        if (field.includeBlank) {
+          var blank = make("option", null, field.blankLabel);
+          blank.value = "";
+          control.appendChild(blank);
+        }
+        field.options.forEach(function (opt) {
+          var option = make("option", null, opt);
+          option.value = opt;
+          control.appendChild(option);
+        });
+        reset = field.default !== undefined
+          ? field.default
+          : (field.includeBlank ? "" : field.options[0]);
+        control.value = reset;
+      } else if (field.kind === "textarea") {
+        control = make("textarea", "form-control");
+        control.rows = 4;
+      } else {
+        control = make("input", "form-control");
+        control.type = field.kind === "date" ? "date" : "text";
+        if (field.placeholder) {
+          control.setAttribute("placeholder", field.placeholder);
+        }
+      }
+      control.setAttribute("data-input", field.name);
+      if (field.required) {
+        control.required = true;
+      }
+      wrap.appendChild(control);
+      form.appendChild(wrap);
+      controls.push({ name: field.name, control: control, reset: reset });
+    });
+    var actions = make("div", "field-editor-actions");
+    var submit = make("button", "button button--primary", spec.submitLabel);
+    submit.type = "submit";
+    actions.appendChild(submit);
+    form.appendChild(actions);
+
+    form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
+      e.preventDefault();
+      var prior = form.querySelector(".error-line");
+      if (prior) {
+        prior.parentNode.removeChild(prior);
+      }
+      if (!form.checkValidity()) {
+        form.reportValidity();
+        return;
+      }
+      var values = {};
+      controls.forEach(function (entry) {
+        values[entry.name] = entry.control.value;
+      });
+      submit.disabled = true;
+      Promise.resolve(onSubmit(values)).then(
+        function () {
+          controls.forEach(function (entry) {
+            entry.control.value = entry.reset;
+          });
+        },
+        function (err) {
+          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
+        }
+      ).then(function () {
+        submit.disabled = false;
+      });
+    });
+    return form;
+  }

+  // Transient conversation-render state (SPEC §11 / §9 no-store doctrine). Chat

+  var chatTranscripts = {};   // entity_id -> [{who: "you"|"planner", text: string}]
+  var chatDrafts = {};        // entity_id -> string (pending, unsent input text)

+  function advanceTarget(state, ceiling) {
+    // Mirror of machine.py:40-48 (+ the ceiling=done special case).
+    if (state === "in_progress" && ceiling === "done") {
+      return "done";
+    }

+    return make("div", "quiet-line", text);

+  // Shared mutating-button discipline (amendment A3): on click clear any prior
+  // error and disable; on rejection re-enable and surface the error; on SUCCESS
+  // stay disabled — the WS-flush re-render replaces the DOM, and re-enabling would
+  // only reopen the flush-gap double-fire window. Private helper (not exported).
+  function bindMutating(button, container, run) {
+    button.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      var prior = container.querySelector(".error-line");
+      if (prior) {
+        container.removeChild(prior);
+      }
+      button.disabled = true;
+      Promise.resolve(run()).then(
+        function () {},
+        function (err) {
+          button.disabled = false;
+          container.prepend(errorLine(err));
+        }
+      );
+    });
+  }

+  // --- the pluggable chat-input source (SPEC §14 audio seam) -----------------
+  // A source is factory(ctx) -> HTMLElement. ctx.submit(text) -> Promise is the
+  // only way a source delivers input (the panel owns transport); ctx.initialText
+  // is the preserved draft; ctx.onInput(text) reports pending text so the panel
+  // can preserve the draft across re-renders.
+  function makeTextInputSource(ctx) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "chat-input");
+    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input chat-input-text");
+    textarea.setAttribute("data-chat-input", "");
+    textarea.rows = 2;
+    textarea.value = ctx.initialText || "";
+    textarea.addEventListener("input", function () {
+      ctx.onInput(textarea.value);
+    });
+    var send = make("button", "button button--primary", "Send");
+    send.type = "button";
+    send.setAttribute("data-chat-send", "");
+    send.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      var text = textarea.value.trim();
+      if (!text) {
+        return;
+      }
+      send.disabled = true;
+      textarea.value = "";
+      ctx.onInput("");
+      Promise.resolve(ctx.submit(text)).then(
+        function () {
+          send.disabled = false;
+        },
+        function () {
+          send.disabled = false;
+        }
+      );
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(textarea);
+    wrap.appendChild(send);
+    return wrap;
+  }

+  // --- D11 item 8: grant-pair picker -----------------------------------------
+  // getGrant() returns null until BOTH halves are explicitly chosen. The ceiling
+  // options are exactly the states resolve_grant accepts (machine.py:94-99): the
+  // NO_FURTHER sentinel plus every STATE_ORDER state at or beyond newState.
+  function grantPairPicker(newState, onChange) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "grant-picker");
+    var label = make("label", "grant-label");
+    label.appendChild(make("span", null, "how far"));
+    var select = make("select", "grant-ceiling");
+    select.setAttribute("data-grant-ceiling", "");
+    var placeholder = make("option", null, "");
+    placeholder.value = "";
+    placeholder.disabled = true;
+    placeholder.selected = true;
+    placeholder.hidden = true;
+    select.appendChild(placeholder);
+    var noFurther = make("option", null, "No further");
+    noFurther.value = "none";
+    select.appendChild(noFurther);
+    var start = STATE_ORDER.indexOf(newState);
+    if (start < 0) {
+      start = 0;
+    }
+    STATE_ORDER.slice(start).forEach(function (state) {
+      var option = make("option", null, state.replace(/_/g, " "));
+      option.value = state;
+      select.appendChild(option);
+    });
+    select.addEventListener("change", onChange);
+    label.appendChild(select);
+    wrap.appendChild(label);
+
+    var atcap = make("span", "grant-atcap");
+    atcap.setAttribute("data-grant-atcap", "");
+    var groupName = "atcap-" + String((_atcapSeq += 1));
+    var inputs = [];
+    [["stop", "Stop"], ["propose", "Propose"]].forEach(function (pair) {
+      var radioLabel = make("label", null);
+      var input = make("input", null);
+      input.type = "radio";
+      input.name = groupName;
+      input.value = pair[0];
+      input.addEventListener("change", onChange);
+      inputs.push(input);
+      radioLabel.appendChild(input);
+      radioLabel.appendChild(make("span", null, pair[1]));
+      atcap.appendChild(radioLabel);
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(atcap);
+
+    wrap.getGrant = function () {
+      if (select.value === "") {
+        return null;
+      }
+      var checked = null;
+      inputs.forEach(function (input) {
+        if (input.checked) {
+          checked = input;
+        }
+      });
+      if (checked === null) {
+        return null;
+      }
+      return { next_ceiling: select.value, at_cap: checked.value };
+    };
+    return wrap;
+  }

+  // --- D11 item 7: proposal card ---------------------------------------------
+  // edited_body is included iff the textarea differs (strict string) from the
+  // proposal body from this render's fetch (resolution.py:151-161). When
+  // requireGrant, Accept is unfireable until the picker yields both halves.
+  function proposalCard(opts) {
