+    card.appendChild(make("div", "proposal-meta", "proposed by " + opts.proposal.proposed_by));
+    card.appendChild(markdownBlock(opts.proposal.body));
+    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input proposal-edit");
+    textarea.setAttribute("data-edit", "");
+    textarea.rows = 8;
+    textarea.value = opts.proposal.body;
+    card.appendChild(textarea);
+
+    var picker = null;
+    var inFlight = false;
+    var resolved = false;
+    var actions = make("div", "proposal-card-actions");
+    var accept = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
+    accept.type = "button";
+    accept.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
+
+    function sync() {
+      accept.disabled = inFlight || resolved ||
+        (opts.requireGrant && (picker === null || picker.getGrant() === null));
+    }
+
+    if (opts.requireGrant) {
+      picker = grantPairPicker(opts.newState, sync);
+      card.appendChild(picker);
+    }
+
+    accept.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");
+      if (prior) {
+        actions.removeChild(prior);
+      }
+      var payload = {};
+      if (textarea.value !== opts.proposal.body) {
+        payload.edited_body = textarea.value;
+      }
+      if (opts.requireGrant) {
+        var grant = picker.getGrant();
+        payload.next_ceiling = grant.next_ceiling;
+        payload.at_cap = grant.at_cap;
+      }
+      inFlight = true;
+      sync();
+      Promise.resolve(opts.onAccept(payload)).then(
+        function () {
+          resolved = true;   // A3: stay disabled after a successful accept
+          inFlight = false;
+          sync();
+        },
+        function (err) {
+          inFlight = false;
+          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
+          sync();
+        }
+      );
+    });
+
+    actions.appendChild(accept);
+    card.appendChild(actions);
+    sync();   // Accept starts disabled when a grant is required (nothing picked)
+    return card;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 9: day-plan tree ---------------------------------------------
+  // node is "root" (string) or the child position (NUMBER — the server rejects
+  // string positions, days/api.py:83-91). No local status mutation: the four
+  // routes append events, so the WS flush re-render shows the new tree.
+  function planTree(plan, handlers) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "plan-tree");
+    var actions = make("div", "plan-tree-actions");
+
+    var acceptAll = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept all");
+    acceptAll.type = "button";
+    acceptAll.setAttribute("data-accept-all", "");
+    bindMutating(acceptAll, actions, function () {
+      return handlers.acceptAll();
+    });
+    var rejectAll = make("button", "button", "Reject all");
+    rejectAll.type = "button";
+    rejectAll.setAttribute("data-reject-all", "");
+    bindMutating(rejectAll, actions, function () {
+      return handlers.rejectAll();
+    });
+    actions.appendChild(acceptAll);
+    actions.appendChild(rejectAll);
+    wrap.appendChild(actions);
+
+    function makeNode(ref, isRoot, label, status, ticketId) {
+      var node = make("div", isRoot ? "plan-node plan-node--root" : "plan-node plan-node--child");
+      node.setAttribute("data-node", isRoot ? "root" : String(ref));
+      node.setAttribute("data-status", status);
+      node.appendChild(make("span", "plan-node-status", status));
+      node.appendChild(make("span", isRoot ? "plan-node-focus" : "plan-node-note", label));
+      if (!isRoot && ticketId) {
+        var link = make("a", "plan-node-ticket", "open");
+        link.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + ticketId);
+        node.appendChild(link);
+      }
+      var nodeActions = make("span", "plan-node-actions");
+      if (status !== "accepted") {   // Accept only offered on a not-yet-accepted node
+        var acceptNode = make("button", "button", "Accept");
+        acceptNode.type = "button";
+        acceptNode.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
+        bindMutating(acceptNode, actions, function () {
+          return handlers.accept(ref);
+        });
+        nodeActions.appendChild(acceptNode);
+      }
+      var invalidate = make("button", "button", "Invalidate");
+      invalidate.type = "button";
+      invalidate.setAttribute("data-invalidate", "");
+      bindMutating(invalidate, actions, function () {
+        return handlers.invalidate(ref);
+      });
+      nodeActions.appendChild(invalidate);
+      node.appendChild(nodeActions);
+      return node;
+    }
+
+    wrap.appendChild(makeNode("root", true, plan.root.focus, plan.root.status, null));
+    (plan.children || []).forEach(function (child) {
+      wrap.appendChild(
+        makeNode(child.position, false, child.note || child.ticket_id, child.status, child.ticket_id)
+      );
+    });
+    return wrap;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 10: chat panel -----------------------------------------------
+  // entityId is the SERVER-provided chat id (day.id / ticket.id), never built
+  // client-side. Rebuilds its message list from the module-scope transcript, which
+  // the WS flush does not touch — that is the survival mechanism.
+  function chatPanel(entityId, opts) {
+    var panelEl = make("div", "chat-panel");
+    panelEl.setAttribute("data-chat-panel", "");
+    var messages = make("div", "chat-messages");
+    messages.setAttribute("data-chat-messages", "");
+    panelEl.appendChild(messages);
+
+    function paint() {
+      messages.replaceChildren();
+      (chatTranscripts[entityId] || []).forEach(function (msg) {
+        if (msg.who === "you") {
+          var you = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--you", msg.text);
+          you.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "you");
+          messages.appendChild(you);
+        } else {
+          var reply = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--planner");
+          reply.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "planner");
+          reply.appendChild(markdownBlock(msg.text));
+          messages.appendChild(reply);
+        }
+      });
+    }
+    paint();
+
+    if (!opts.available) {
+      var offline = make("div", "chat-offline", "gateway offline");
+      offline.setAttribute("data-chat-offline", "");
+      panelEl.appendChild(offline);
+      return panelEl;   // §11 notice; no input source rendered when offline
+    }
+
+    var source;
+    var ctx = {
+      initialText: chatDrafts[entityId] || "",
+      onInput: function (text) {
+        chatDrafts[entityId] = text;
+      },
+      submit: function (text) {
+        if (!chatTranscripts[entityId]) {
+          chatTranscripts[entityId] = [];
+        }
+        chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "you", text: text });
+        delete chatDrafts[entityId];
+        paint();
+        return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + entityId + "/send", {
+          method: "POST",
+          body: { text: text }
+        }).then(
+          function (res) {
+            chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "planner", text: res.reply_text });
+            if (panelEl.isConnected) {   // a flush may have replaced the panel mid-flight
+              paint();
+            }
+            return res;
+          },
+          function (err) {
+            var prior = panelEl.querySelector(".error-line");
+            if (prior) {
+              panelEl.removeChild(prior);
+            }
+            panelEl.insertBefore(errorLine(err), source);
+            return Promise.reject(err);
+          }
+        );
+      }
+    };
+    source = Planner.chatInput.sources[Planner.chatInput.active](ctx);
+    panelEl.appendChild(source);
+    return panelEl;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 16: review card ----------------------------------------------
+  // The shell is panel() (D11 #2 — the only box primitive). The screen guards
+  // staleness before constructing (proposal null / state moved on).
+  function reviewCard(opts) {
+    var entry = opts.entry;
+    var detail = opts.detail;
+    var kind = entry.kind;
+    var gating = kind === "success" || kind === "approach" ||
+      kind === "plan" || kind === "result";
+    var content = [];
+    var head = make("div", "review-card-head");
+    var footer = make("div", "review-card-actions");
+    var primary = null;
+
+    if (gating) {
+      head.appendChild(chip("state", detail.state));
+      head.appendChild(chip("pending-proposal"));
+      content.push(head);
+      content.push(proposalCard({
+        proposal: detail.fields[kind].proposal,
+        requireGrant: true,
+        newState: advanceTarget(detail.state, detail.ceiling),
+        onAccept: opts.onAccept
+      }));
+    } else if (kind === "review") {
+      head.appendChild(chip("state", detail.state));
+      content.push(head);
+      content.push(markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.value));
+      if (detail.fields.result.notes) {
+        var note = make("div", "review-note");
+        note.appendChild(markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.notes));
+        content.push(note);
+      }
+      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Approve");
+      primary.type = "button";
+      primary.setAttribute("data-approve", "");
+      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
+        return opts.onApprove();
+      });
+    } else if (kind === "status") {
+      head.appendChild(chip(null, detail.status));
+      head.appendChild(chip(null, "→ " + detail.status_proposal.to_status));
+      content.push(head);
+      if (detail.status_proposal.note) {
+        content.push(make("div", "review-note", detail.status_proposal.note));
+      }
+      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
+      primary.type = "button";
+      primary.setAttribute("data-accept-status", "");
+      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
+        return opts.onAcceptStatus();
+      });
+    }
+
+    if (primary) {
+      footer.appendChild(primary);
+    }
+    var skip = make("button", "button", "Skip");
+    skip.type = "button";
+    skip.setAttribute("data-skip", "");
+    skip.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      opts.onSkip();
+    });
+    footer.appendChild(skip);
+    if (entry.entity_type === "ticket") {
+      var open = make("a", "review-open", "open ticket");
+      open.setAttribute("data-open-ticket", "");
+      open.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + entry.entity_id);
+      footer.appendChild(open);
+    }
+    content.push(footer);
+
+    var section = panel(entry.title, content);
+    section.setAttribute("data-review-card", "");
+    section.setAttribute("data-entity-id", entry.entity_id);
+    section.setAttribute("data-kind", kind);
+    if (gating) {
+      section.setAttribute("data-field", kind);
+    }
+    return section;
+  }
+
   Planner.components = {
     appShell: appShell,
     panel: panel,

+    planTree: planTree,
+    chatPanel: chatPanel,
+    reviewCard: reviewCard
+  };

+  // registers a second source with one <script> tag and no edits here: chatPanel

+      }

   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")

     1	"""State-machine math for §4.2/§4.3/§4.4: linear order indexing, gating and
     2	advance tables, the auto-accept condition, ceiling comparison, and onward-grant
     3	resolution. Pure: contracts/errors + fields_codec only."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
     8	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
     9	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    10	    GATING_FIELD,
    11	    NO_FURTHER,
    12	    STATE_ORDER,
    13	    AtCap,
    14	    FieldName,
    15	    GrantPair,
    16	    NextCeiling,
    17	    TicketFields,
    18	    TicketState,
    19	)
    20	from planner.tickets.logic import fields_codec
    21	
    22	
    23	def state_index(state: TicketState) -> int:
    24	    try:
    25	        return STATE_ORDER.index(state)
    26	    except ValueError as exc:
    27	        raise PlannerError(
    28	            ErrorCode.validation, "state outside the linear order", {"state": state.value}
    29	        ) from exc
    30	
    31	
    32	def is_terminal(state: TicketState) -> bool:
    33	    return state in (TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped)
    34	
    35	
    36	def gating_field(state: TicketState) -> FieldName | None:
    37	    return GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    38	
    39	
    40	def advance_target(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> TicketState:
    41	    if state is TicketState.in_progress and ceiling is TicketState.done:
    42	        return TicketState.done
    43	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET.get(state)
    44	    if target is None:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.validation, "state has no advance target", {"state": state.value}
    47	        )
    48	    return target
    49	
    50	
    51	def auto_accept_target(
    52	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, field: FieldName
    53	) -> TicketState | None:
    54	    if is_terminal(state):
    55	        return None
    56	    if field is not gating_field(state):
    57	        return None
    58	    target = advance_target(state, ceiling)
    59	    if state_index(target) > state_index(ceiling):
    60	        return None
    61	    return target
    62	
    63	
    64	def at_or_beyond_ceiling(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> bool:
    65	    return state_index(state) >= state_index(ceiling)
    66	
    67	
    68	def validate_ceiling(ceiling: TicketState) -> None:
    69	    if ceiling not in STATE_ORDER:
    70	        raise PlannerError(
    71	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "ceiling must be a linear state", {"ceiling": ceiling.value}
    72	        )
    73	
    74	
    75	def resolve_grant(
    76	    new_state: TicketState, next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None, at_cap: AtCap | None
    77	) -> GrantPair:
    78	    if next_ceiling is None or at_cap is None:
    79	        missing: list[str] = []
    80	        if next_ceiling is None:
    81	            missing.append("next_ceiling")
    82	        if at_cap is None:
    83	            missing.append("at_cap")
    84	        raise PlannerError(
    85	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "accept requires the onward grant pair", {"missing": missing}
    86	        )
    87	    if next_ceiling == NO_FURTHER:
    88	        # the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state
    89	        return GrantPair(next_ceiling=new_state, at_cap=at_cap)
    90	    if not isinstance(next_ceiling, TicketState):
    91	        raise PlannerError(
    92	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": str(next_ceiling)}
    93	        )
    94	    if next_ceiling not in STATE_ORDER or state_index(next_ceiling) < state_index(new_state):
    95	        raise PlannerError(
    96	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid,
    97	            "next_ceiling must be at or beyond the new state",
    98	            {"next_ceiling": next_ceiling.value, "new_state": new_state.value},
    99	        )
   100	    return GrantPair(next_ceiling=next_ceiling, at_cap=at_cap)
   101	
   102	
   103	def has_pending_gating_proposal(state: TicketState, fields: TicketFields) -> bool:
   104	    field = gating_field(state)
   105	    if field is None:
   106	        return False
   107	    return fields_codec.get_slot(fields, field).proposal is not None

    84	def approvals_digest(
    85	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]]
    86	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    87	    """§6.2 step 3 / §4.5 approval-queue digest. Three sources, oldest-pending first:
    88	
    89	    * a ticket with a non-null proposal on its current gating field
    90	      (GATING_FIELD[state]); kind = the gating field name (e.g. "plan"),
    91	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"];
    92	    * a ticket in ``needs_review`` (§4.5); kind = "review", waiting_since =
    93	      the ticket's updated_at (DB-internal proxy for review entry time);
    94	    * a sprint item with a non-null status_proposal; kind = "status",
    95	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"].
    96	
    97	    Digest per base.py: {entity_id, kind, waiting_since}. Ticket rows carry
    98	    {id, state, fields(dict), updated_at}; item rows carry {id, status_proposal}.
    99	    """
   100	    digest: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   101	    for row in tickets:
   102	        state = row["state"]
   103	        if state == TicketState.needs_review:
   104	            digest.append(
   105	                {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "review", "waiting_since": row["updated_at"]}
   106	            )
   107	            continue
   108	        gating = GATING_FIELD.get(TicketState(state))
   109	        if gating is None:
   110	            continue
   111	        slot = row["fields"].get(gating.value)
   112	        if slot is None:
   113	            continue
   114	        proposal = slot.get("proposal")
   115	        if proposal is None:
   116	            continue
   117	        digest.append(
   118	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": gating.value, "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   119	        )
   120	    for row in items:
   121	        proposal = row["status_proposal"]
   122	        if proposal is None:
   123	            continue
   124	        digest.append(
   125	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "status", "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   126	        )
   127	    digest.sort(key=lambda entry: entry["waiting_since"])
   128	    return digest

   150	    if raw is None:
   151	        return None

   153	        return NO_FURTHER

   156	    except ValueError:

   265	def _entity_type(entity_id: str) -> str:

   269	def _approvals(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict]) -> list[JsonDict]:
   270	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   271	        "SELECT id, title, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets "
   272	        "WHERE state NOT IN ('done','dropped') ORDER BY id"
   273	    ).fetchall()
   274	    ticket_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []

   278	        tid = str(r["id"])
   279	        ticket_digest.append(

   284	                "updated_at": int(r["updated_at"]),
   285	            }

   293	        item_digest.append({"id": iid, "status_proposal": r["status_proposal"]})
   294	        item_title[iid] = str(r["title"])
   295	    digest = approvals_digest(ticket_digest, item_digest)

   297	    # updated_at proxy; the boundary digest keeps its documented proxy.

   304	                (tid,),
   305	            ).fetchone()
   306	            entry["waiting_since"] = (
   307	                int(row["created_at"]) if row is not None
   308	                else ticket_updated.get(tid, entry["waiting_since"])
   309	            )
   310	    digest.sort(key=lambda e: e["waiting_since"])
   311	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   312	    for entry in digest:
   313	        eid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   314	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   315	        title = ticket_title.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_title.get(eid)
   316	        result.append(
   317	            {
   318	                "entity_id": eid,
   319	                "entity_type": etype,
   320	                "kind": entry["kind"],
   321	                "title": title,
   322	                "waiting_since": entry["waiting_since"],
   323	            }
   324	        )
   325	    return result

     1	/* Minimal safe markdown renderer. The ONLY innerHTML assignment in the whole
     2	 * codebase lives here — and it receives a string whose every character of user
     3	 * origin was HTML-escaped BEFORE any parsing, so no un-entified "<", ">", or quote
     4	 * can exist. Every tag is renderer-authored from a closed set; the only attribute
     5	 * emitted is a scheme-whitelisted href. Classic script: attaches Planner.markdown. */
     6	(function () {
     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
     9	
    10	  // Code-span placeholder sentinel (NUL). Stripped from raw input first, so a
    11	  // user string can never forge a placeholder.
    12	  var NUL = String.fromCharCode(0);
    13	  var RESTORE_RE = new RegExp(NUL + "(\\d+)" + NUL, "g");
    14	
    15	  function escapeHtml(s) {
    16	    return s
    17	      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    18	      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    19	      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    20	      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    21	      .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
    22	  }
    23	
    24	  var SCHEME_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/;
    25	
    26	  function safeHref(url) {
    27	    // Validate a normalized copy: browsers strip leading C0 controls/spaces
    28	    // (and interior tab/newline) when parsing URLs, so "\u0001javascript:..."
    29	    // would read as scheme-less here yet execute after normalization. Our strip
    30	    // is a superset of the browser's, so anything passing this check cannot
    31	    // normalize to a non-whitelisted scheme.
    32	    var norm = url.replace(/[\u0000-\u0020]+/g, "").toLowerCase();
    33	    if (SCHEME_RE.test(norm)) {
    34	      return (
    35	        norm.indexOf("http:") === 0 ||
    36	        norm.indexOf("https:") === 0 ||
    37	        norm.indexOf("mailto:") === 0
    38	      );
    39	    }
    40	    return true; // scheme-less (relative, "#/ticket/...", "?", "/") is allowed
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  function renderInline(line) {
    44	    var codes = [];
    45	    // 1. Code spans first — protected from all further processing.
    46	    line = line.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, function (match, inner) {
    47	      var index = codes.length;
    48	      codes.push(inner);
    49	      return NUL + index + NUL;
    50	    });
    51	    // 2. Links — href is scheme-whitelisted; the value is already escaped.
    52	    line = line.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^()\s]+)\)/g, function (match, text, url) {
    53	      if (safeHref(url)) {
    54	        return '<a href="' + url + '">' + text + "</a>";
    55	      }
    56	      return match;
    57	    });
    58	    // 3. Bold before italic so "**" is not eaten by "*".
    59	    line = line.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, "<strong>$1</strong>");
    60	    // 4. Italic.
    61	    line = line.replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g, "<em>$1</em>");
    62	    line = line.replace(/_([^_]+)_/g, "<em>$1</em>");
    63	    // 5. Restore code spans.
    64	    line = line.replace(RESTORE_RE, function (match, index) {
    65	      return "<code>" + codes[Number(index)] + "</code>";
    66	    });
    67	    return line;
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  function render(text) {
    71	    // 1. Strip the placeholder sentinel so collisions are impossible.
    72	    text = String(text).split(NUL).join("");
    73	    // 2. Escape everything once, before any parsing.
    74	    var escaped = escapeHtml(text);
    75	    var lines = escaped.split("\n");
    76	    var out = [];
    77	    var para = null;
    78	    var listType = null;
    79	    var listItems = null;
    80	    var i = 0;
    81	
    82	    function closePara() {
    83	      if (para) {
    84	        out.push("<p>" + para.join("<br>") + "</p>");
    85	        para = null;
    86	      }
    87	    }
    88	    function closeList() {
    89	      if (listItems) {
    90	        out.push("<" + listType + ">" + listItems.join("") + "</" + listType + ">");
    91	        listItems = null;
    92	        listType = null;
    93	      }
    94	    }
    95	    function closeBlocks() {
    96	      closePara();
    97	      closeList();
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    while (i < lines.length) {
   101	      var line = lines[i];
   102	      // Fenced code block — verbatim, no inline processing (already escaped).
   103	      if (/^```/.test(line)) {
   104	        closeBlocks();
   105	        i += 1;
   106	        var codeLines = [];
   107	        while (i < lines.length && !/^```/.test(lines[i])) {
   108	          codeLines.push(lines[i]);
   109	          i += 1;
   110	        }
   111	        if (i < lines.length) {
   112	          i += 1; // consume the closing fence
   113	        }
   114	        out.push("<pre><code>" + codeLines.join("\n") + "</code></pre>");
   115	        continue;
   116	      }
   117	      // Blank line closes the open block.
   118	      if (/^\s*$/.test(line)) {
   119	        closeBlocks();
   120	        i += 1;
   121	        continue;
   122	      }
   123	      // Heading — caps at h3 (four or more "#" render as h3).
   124	      var heading = /^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   125	      if (heading) {
   126	        closeBlocks();
   127	        var level = heading[1].length;
   128	        if (level > 3) {
   129	          level = 3;
   130	        }
   131	        out.push("<h" + level + ">" + renderInline(heading[2]) + "</h" + level + ">");
   132	        i += 1;
   133	        continue;
   134	      }
   135	      // Unordered list item.
   136	      var unordered = /^[-*+]\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   137	      if (unordered) {
   138	        closePara();
   139	        if (listType && listType !== "ul") {
   140	          closeList();
   141	        }
   142	        if (!listItems) {
   143	          listType = "ul";
   144	          listItems = [];
   145	        }
   146	        listItems.push("<li>" + renderInline(unordered[1]) + "</li>");
   147	        i += 1;
   148	        continue;
   149	      }
   150	      // Ordered list item.

    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)
    63

    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }

    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )

   214	    if not body:
   215	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no item fields to update", {})
   216	    if "blocked_by" in body and "status" not in body:
   217	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "blocked_by requires status", {})
   218	    for field in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   219	        if field in body:
   220	            if field == "deadline":
   221	                _marshal_item_deadline(body["deadline"])
   222	            sprints_data.update_item_field(conn, item_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   223	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   224	        sprints_data.assign_item_sprint(conn, item_id, body["sprint_id"], clock=clk)
   225	    if "status" in body:
   226	        to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body["status"], "status")
   227	        sprints_data.transition_item_status(
   228	            conn, item_id, to_status, clock=clk, by_agent=not ctx.is_human,
   229	            blocked_by=body.get("blocked_by"),
   230	        )
   231	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   232	
   233	
   234	@router.post("/items/{item_id}/propose-status")
   235	async def propose_item_status(item_id: str, body: ProposeStatusBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   236	                              clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   237	    to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body.to, "status")
   238	    sprints_data.propose_item_status(
   239	        conn, item_id, to_status, note=body.note, proposed_by=ctx.actor, clock=clk
   240	    )
   241	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   242	
   243	
   244	@router.post("/items/{item_id}/accept-status")
   245	async def accept_item_status(item_id: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   246	    reject_agents(ctx)
   247	    sprints_data.accept_item_status(conn, item_id, clock=clk, resolved_by="human")
   248	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   249	
   250	
   251	# --- sprint routes -------------------------------------------------------------
   252

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/planner/days/logic/tree.py | sed -n '1,180p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Plan-tree transforms (§6.3) as pure functions ``PlanTree → (new_tree, effects)``,
     2	plus JSON serialization of the tree for the ``days.plan`` column and for event
     3	payloads that carry ``old_tree``. No side effects; the data layer persists the
     4	returned tree and applies the effects. Stdlib + planner contract modules only."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	from dataclasses import replace
     9	
    10	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict
    11	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanRoot, PlanTree
    12	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    13	    AddTicketToDay,
    14	    Effect,
    15	    EmitEvent,
    16	    ReplanChild,
    17	    ReplanRoot,
    18	)
    19	
    20	# A node address in the plan tree: the literal ``"root"`` or a child ``position``.
    21	# Mirrors the plan_node_accepted / plan_node_invalidated payload contract, whose
    22	# ``{node}`` value is ``"root" | <child position>``.
    23	NodeRef = str | int
    24	
    25	
    26	def tree_to_dict(tree: PlanTree) -> JsonDict:
    27	    """{root:{focus,status}, children:[{ticket_id,note,status,position}, ...]}.
    28	
    29	    NodeStatus serialized as its str value; child order preserved.
    30	    """
    31	    return {
    32	        "root": {"focus": tree.root.focus, "status": tree.root.status.value},
    33	        "children": [
    34	            {
    35	                "ticket_id": child.ticket_id,
    36	                "note": child.note,
    37	                "status": child.status.value,
    38	                "position": child.position,
    39	            }
    40	            for child in tree.children
    41	        ],
    42	    }
    43	
    44	
    45	def tree_from_dict(data: JsonDict) -> PlanTree:
    46	    """Inverse of ``tree_to_dict``. status parsed via ``NodeStatus(...)``."""
    47	    root = data["root"]
    48	    children = [
    49	        PlanNode(
    50	            ticket_id=child["ticket_id"],
    51	            note=child["note"],
    52	            status=NodeStatus(child["status"]),
    53	            position=child["position"],
    54	        )
    55	        for child in data["children"]
    56	    ]
    57	    return PlanTree(
    58	        root=PlanRoot(focus=root["focus"], status=NodeStatus(root["status"])),
    59	        children=children,
    60	    )
    61	
    62	
    63	def as_proposed(tree: PlanTree) -> PlanTree:
    64	    """Return a copy with root and every child status=proposed. The boundary
    65	    stores the judgment tree as a PROPOSED plan (§6.2)."""
    66	    return PlanTree(
    67	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.proposed),
    68	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.proposed) for child in tree.children],
    69	    )
    70	
    71	
    72	def accept_node(tree: PlanTree, ref: NodeRef) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
    73	    """§6.3: accepting a node sets it accepted; accepting root does NOT cascade.
    74	
    75	    A child with a ticket_id also yields an AddTicketToDay effect (appended at
    76	    end if absent). Effect order: EmitEvent(plan_node_accepted) then (for a child
    77	    with a ticket_id) AddTicketToDay.
    78	    """
    79	    if ref == "root":
    80	        new_tree = PlanTree(
    81	            root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
    82	            children=[replace(child) for child in tree.children],
    83	        )
    84	        return new_tree, [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": "root"})]
    85	
    86	    accepted: PlanNode | None = None
    87	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
    88	    for child in tree.children:
    89	        if child.position == ref:
    90	            accepted = replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted)
    91	            children.append(accepted)
    92	        else:
    93	            children.append(replace(child))
    94	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
    95	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": ref})]
    96	    if accepted is not None and accepted.ticket_id:
    97	        effects.append(AddTicketToDay(accepted.ticket_id))
    98	    return new_tree, effects
    99	
   100	
   101	def accept_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   102	    """§6.3 accept-all: root + every child accepted. Effects =
   103	    [EmitEvent(plan_accepted_all, {})] then AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id) for
   104	    each child with a ticket_id, in child position order. Idempotent add is the
   105	    data layer's job (a17)."""
   106	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   107	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
   108	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted) for child in tree.children],
   109	    )
   110	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_accepted_all, {})]
   111	    for child in tree.children:
   112	        if child.ticket_id:
   113	            effects.append(AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id))
   114	    return new_tree, effects
   115	
   116	
   117	def invalidate_root(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   118	    """§6.3: root + EVERY child → invalidated; request exactly one root replan."""
   119	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   120	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.invalidated),
   121	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated) for child in tree.children],
   122	    )
   123	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   124	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": "root"}),
   125	        ReplanRoot(),
   126	    ]
   127	    return new_tree, effects
   128	
   129	
   130	def invalidate_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   131	    """§6.3: ONLY the child at ``position`` → invalidated; root and every other
   132	    child keep their prior status. Requests exactly one child replan."""
   133	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
   134	    for child in tree.children:
   135	        if child.position == position:
   136	            children.append(replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated))
   137	        else:
   138	            children.append(replace(child))
   139	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
   140	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   141	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": position}),
   142	        ReplanChild(position),
   143	    ]
   144	    return new_tree, effects
   145	
   146	
   147	def reject_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[None, list[Effect]]:
   148	    """§6.3 reject-all: plan cleared. New tree is None (the data layer writes
   149	    days.plan = NULL). The old tree is preserved in the event payload."""
   150	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   151	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_rejected, {"old_tree": tree_to_dict(tree)})
   152	    ]
   153	    return None, effects

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:1586:    85	- The agent grant is two things, always together: **how far** (`ceiling`) and **what happens there** (`at_cap`). These are the whole vocabulary of agent permission.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:1587:    86	- `ceiling` is the highest state an agent-caused advancement may move the ticket **into**. Below the ceiling, agents work freely: proposals on the current gating field auto-accept and advance (Section 4.4). Human actions ignore the ceiling entirely (the human may jump a ticket to any state, including straight to `done`; skipped gating fields simply keep `value = null`).
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:1588:    87	- `at_cap` governs the ticket once it sits at its ceiling: `stop` — agents may not file any proposal; the ticket rests untouched until the human changes the grant. `propose` — agents may file a proposal on the current gating field only; it parks for the human; nothing advances.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:1601:   100	7. **The onward grant.** Human acceptance of a gating-field proposal (accept or edit-accept) requires the explicit grant pair: `next_ceiling` — any state at or beyond the newly entered state, or `none` meaning the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state — AND `at_cap` (`stop`: agents don't even propose from here; `propose`: agents may draft the next gating field for approval). Both are stated on every accept, even when the answer is "nothing further"; an accept without the pair is rejected with a structured error. Agent auto-accepts change neither ceiling nor at_cap. The `needs_review` approve action and sprint-item status accepts carry no onward grant (they are terminal for agent involvement).
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:1632:   131	If the human has already planned the day explicitly (any day-ticket or accepted plan exists) before the boundary job's judgment pass, the judgment pass is skipped entirely.

Human writes happen through the UI/API only — the CLI is an agent tool. A write with a stale/foreign claim is rejected with a structured error naming the mismatch.

Global behavior: `--json` on every verb (machine output; stable exit codes: 0 success, 1 validation/domain error, 2 connection error); long text via `--body-file <path>` or `-` for stdin — body text is never passed as an inline argument; env defaults `PLAN_SERVER_URL`, `PLAN_TICKET_ID` (verbs taking a ticket id use it when omitted), `PLAN_RUN_ID`, `PLAN_CLAIM`, `PLAN_ACTOR`.

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:2267:   351	- **Skip memory, precisely:** `skipped` is module-scope, keyed `entity_id + ":" + kind` (an entity can legitimately reappear later under a different kind — a skip must not hide the new kind). Skip = `skipped[key] = true` + re-render (`rerender()` clears the root and calls `render(root)` again — Skip is client-only, no server event, so no flush will do it for us). Transient and per-session by construction: a reload re-executes the script, resetting `skipped = {}` — after reload the oldest entry is first again. **Wrap rule:** when every queued entry is skipped, the skip set is cleared and the walk restarts at the oldest (no dead-end, no instructional message).

The two failure paths become:
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:2528:   612	- **Detail-fetch rejection** (transient or otherwise): do NOT hide the entry and do NOT auto-refetch. Render an inline error surface in the screen root: `errorLine(err)`, a quiet line with `entry.title`, and a `button.button [data-skip] "Skip"` whose click does `skipped[key(entry)] = true; rerender();` — plus, for tickets, the `[data-open-ticket]` anchor. The human decides to move on; nothing is hidden for the session; no fetch loop is possible (nothing refetches until the human acts or a flush lands).
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/impl-review-raw.txt:2529:   613	- **Staleness guard** (detail fetched fine but the entry no longer matches: gating proposal null / gating field mismatch / state left needs_review / status_proposal null): if `skipped[key(entry)]` is NOT yet set — `skipped[key(entry)] = true; rerender();` (the fresh `/api/queues` in that rerender will almost always have dropped the entry). If it IS already set (i.e., the wrap rule re-surfaced an entry that is stale again — the only way a loop could start): render the A1 empty quiet line and stop. Bounded, transient, nothing permanently hidden — a reload or the next flush re-derives everything from the server.

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:930:**Anti-shortcut clause:** The plan file is the OUTPUT of the interactive review, not a substitute for it. Writing every finding into one plan write and calling ExitPlanMode without firing AskUserQuestion is the precise failure mode of the May 2026 transcript bug — the model explored, found issues, and dumped them into a deliverable rather than walking the user through them. If you have ANY non-trivial finding in any review section, the path from finding to ExitPlanMode goes THROUGH AskUserQuestion. Zero findings in every section is the only path to ExitPlanMode that bypasses AskUserQuestion. If you find yourself wanting to write a plan with findings before asking, stop and call AskUserQuestion now — that's the bug, recognize it.

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2021:   351	- **Skip memory, precisely:** `skipped` is module-scope, keyed `entity_id + ":" + kind` (an entity can legitimately reappear later under a different kind — a skip must not hide the new kind). Skip = `skipped[key] = true` + re-render (`rerender()` clears the root and calls `render(root)` again — Skip is client-only, no server event, so no flush will do it for us). Transient and per-session by construction: a reload re-executes the script, resetting `skipped = {}` — after reload the oldest entry is first again. **Wrap rule:** when every queued entry is skipped, the skip set is cleared and the walk restarts at the oldest (no dead-end, no instructional message).
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2024:   354	  2. `fetchJson(path + entry.entity_id)` → on **rejection** (entity vanished between queue fetch and detail fetch): `vanished[key(entry)] = true; rerender();` — `vanished` is separate from `skipped` and is never cleared by the wrap rule, so a genuinely broken entry cannot cause a fetch loop; the next queue refetch drops truly-resolved entries anyway.

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2395:    85	- The agent grant is two things, always together: **how far** (`ceiling`) and **what happens there** (`at_cap`). These are the whole vocabulary of agent permission.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2396:    86	- `ceiling` is the highest state an agent-caused advancement may move the ticket **into**. Below the ceiling, agents work freely: proposals on the current gating field auto-accept and advance (Section 4.4). Human actions ignore the ceiling entirely (the human may jump a ticket to any state, including straight to `done`; skipped gating fields simply keep `value = null`).
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2397:    87	- `at_cap` governs the ticket once it sits at its ceiling: `stop` — agents may not file any proposal; the ticket rests untouched until the human changes the grant. `propose` — agents may file a proposal on the current gating field only; it parks for the human; nothing advances.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2410:   100	7. **The onward grant.** Human acceptance of a gating-field proposal (accept or edit-accept) requires the explicit grant pair: `next_ceiling` — any state at or beyond the newly entered state, or `none` meaning the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state — AND `at_cap` (`stop`: agents don't even propose from here; `propose`: agents may draft the next gating field for approval). Both are stated on every accept, even when the answer is "nothing further"; an accept without the pair is rejected with a structured error. Agent auto-accepts change neither ceiling nor at_cap. The `needs_review` approve action and sprint-item status accepts carry no onward grant (they are terminal for agent involvement).

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2395:    85	- The agent grant is two things, always together: **how far** (`ceiling`) and **what happens there** (`at_cap`). These are the whole vocabulary of agent permission.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2396:    86	- `ceiling` is the highest state an agent-caused advancement may move the ticket **into**. Below the ceiling, agents work freely: proposals on the current gating field auto-accept and advance (Section 4.4). Human actions ignore the ceiling entirely (the human may jump a ticket to any state, including straight to `done`; skipped gating fields simply keep `value = null`).
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2397:    87	- `at_cap` governs the ticket once it sits at its ceiling: `stop` — agents may not file any proposal; the ticket rests untouched until the human changes the grant. `propose` — agents may file a proposal on the current gating field only; it parks for the human; nothing advances.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan-review-raw.txt:2410:   100	7. **The onward grant.** Human acceptance of a gating-field proposal (accept or edit-accept) requires the explicit grant pair: `next_ceiling` — any state at or beyond the newly entered state, or `none` meaning the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state — AND `at_cap` (`stop`: agents don't even propose from here; `propose`: agents may draft the next gating field for approval). Both are stated on every accept, even when the answer is "nothing further"; an accept without the pair is rejected with a structured error. Agent auto-accepts change neither ceiling nor at_cap. The `needs_review` approve action and sprint-item status accepts carry no onward grant (they are terminal for agent involvement).

codex
There is an existing raw review artifact in the ticket folder, but I’m not treating it as source of truth for this pass. I’m only using the ticket/plan/spec/server code and the scoped implementation you asked for.

codex
[P2] `assets/screens-review.js:101` — Stale wrap can dead-end the Review screen. After all entries are skipped, `render` clears `skipped` and calls `show(..., wrapped=true)` (`assets/screens-review.js:33-39`). If the oldest entry is stale again, `show` renders `data-review-empty` immediately (`assets/screens-review.js:98-103`) instead of advancing past that stale entry. That can hide later live approvals from the same `/api/queues` payload until reload/flush, violating A2’s no-dead-end/no-hiding intent.

[P3] `orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/smoke.py:195` — Smoke weakens the plan’s data-node assertion. Plan §7(a) requires child plan rows to be exactly `"0"` and `"1"` (`plan.md:496`), but the smoke only asserts child count and `data-status` (`smoke.py:195-196`). A regression that renders two child rows with wrong `data-node` values would pass, despite the §6 data-* contract depending on those positions.

Note: full `smoke.py` was not runnable in this read-only sandbox because it creates temp DB/log files; JS syntax checks passed.

VIOLATIONS: 2.
